public-medium-02

Analog Batch Processing

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 17 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# Analog Batch Processing

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

Write a PLC function block that processes analog inputs in batches and converts them into engineering values.

1. analogValues supplies the raw-value array. channels supplies per-channel settings and receives conversion results. count specifies how many channels to process from the beginning of the arrays. The caller must provide count from zero through the smaller of the two array lengths. Match channels by ordinal position starting at each array's own lower bound. Read analogValues without modifying it.
2. Each channel is enabled by its enable field and converted according to measuringMode. Read configuration fields without modifying them; write only retVal and outputValue.
3. For measuringMode 0, perform non-temperature linear scaling: when bipolar is FALSE, map raw values 0..27648 to loLIM..hiLIM; when TRUE, map -27648..27648 to loLIM..hiLIM.
4. For measuringMode 1, perform standard-temperature conversion: outputValue is one tenth of the raw analog value.
5. For measuringMode 2, perform climatic-temperature conversion: outputValue is one hundredth of the raw analog value.
6. If measuringMode is not 0, 1 or 2, set that channel's retVal to 16#8001 and skip its conversion.
7. If hiLIM is less than or equal to loLIM, set that channel's retVal to 16#8002 and skip its conversion.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `count` | `DINT` | Number of channels to be converted |
| Input/Output | `analogValues` | `ARRAY[*] OF INT` | Variable-length analog array passed by reference to preserve its bounds. Read-only to the function block; no element may be modified. |
| Input/Output | `channels` | `ARRAY[*] OF ST_AnalogChannel` | Channel records passed by reference. Configuration fields are read-only; write only retVal and outputValue. |
| Input field | `channels[].enable` | `BOOL` | Channel conversion enable. Read-only to the function block. |
| Input field | `channels[].hiLIM` | `REAL` | Upper engineering-unit limit. Read-only to the function block. |
| Input field | `channels[].loLIM` | `REAL` | Lower engineering-unit limit. Read-only to the function block. |
| Input field | `channels[].bipolar` | `BOOL` | FALSE selects unipolar and TRUE selects bipolar operation. Read-only to the function block. |
| Input field | `channels[].measuringMode` | `INT` | 0 non-temperature, 1 standard temperature, 2 climatic temperature. Read-only to the function block. |
| Output field | `channels[].retVal` | `WORD` | Per-channel status code written by the function block. |
| Output field | `channels[].outputValue` | `REAL` | Converted value written by the function block. |

Rows named `channels[].fieldName` describe the element fields of ST_AnalogChannel, rather than additional function-block parameters.

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
countinputDINTNumber of channels to be converted
analogValuesinoutARRAY[*] OF INTVariable-length analog array passed by reference to preserve its bounds. Read-only to the function block; no element may be modified.
channelsinoutARRAY[*] OF ST_AnalogChannelChannel records passed by reference. Configuration fields are read-only; write only retVal and outputValue.
channels[].enableinputBOOLChannel conversion enable. Read-only to the function block. |
channels[].hiLIMinputREALUpper engineering-unit limit. Read-only to the function block. |
channels[].loLIMinputREALLower engineering-unit limit. Read-only to the function block. |
channels[].bipolarinputBOOLFALSE selects unipolar and TRUE selects bipolar operation. Read-only to the function block. |
channels[].measuringModeinputINT0 non-temperature, 1 standard temperature, 2 climatic temperature. Read-only to the function block. |
channels[].retValoutputWORDPer-channel status code written by the function block. |
channels[].outputValueoutputREALConverted value written by the function block. |

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S5全部原始数据、通道配置和选中前缀以外的整条通道保持不变。count 为 0 时所有通道保持。
G(__spec_analog_preserve)
safetybounded_checker
S6选中的禁用通道保持全部字段。
G(__spec_analog_disabled)
safetybounded_checker
S1analogValues 是只读输入量，任何周期都不得被改写；未使能通道的 retVal/outputValue 必须保持原值。—safetysimulation
S2measuringMode 不在 0/1/2 时该通道 retVal=16#8001 且跳过转换；hiLIM<=loLIM 时 retVal=16#8002 且跳过转换。—safetysimulation
S3mode0 线性标定：bipolar=FALSE 时 0..27648 线性映射到 loLIM..hiLIM，bipolar=TRUE 时 -27648..27648 映射到 loLIM..hiLIM。—datasimulation
S4mode1 标准温度 outputValue=raw/10，mode2 气候温度 outputValue=raw/100；count 指定从各数组下界起处理的通道数并按序号对应。—datasimulation
S7选中且启用的通道遇到坏模式时返回 8001，量程无效时返回 8002，两错同时出现允许任一适用错误码；跳过转换。
G(__spec_analog_errors)
safetybounded_checker
S8按各数组各自的下界对齐序号，选中且启用、配置有效的每个通道执行指定换算，误差采用功能入口的容差。
G(__spec_analog_conversion)
safetybounded_checker

功能用例

默认组 · single · 每条使用新实例 · 16 条

用例扫描拍数初态／当条预置常量输入当条输入必需期望 expected允许的输出组合 expected_any（任选一组）断言范围说明

public-medium-03

Calculate Moving Average

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 21 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# Calculate Moving Average

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

Write a function block FB that calculates and updates a moving arithmetic mean. The moving arithmetic mean is a method of averaging over consecutive data points, where each new data point replaces the oldest data point, and then the average is recalculated.

1. When cyclicExecution is TRUE, a new data value is automatically read and the moving average average is updated every scan cycle.
2. Provide an external trigger signal trigger, which reads a new data value and updates the moving average upon each rising edge.
3. Provide a reset function reset, which resets the moving average calculation when the reset signal is TRUE. If the window size is less than 1 or greater than 100, output an error status and status code.

The status parameter indicates the execution status of the program:
- 16#0000: Execution successful
- 16#8200: Window length setting error, please set a value between 1 and 100.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `cyclicExecution` | `BOOL` | When TRUE, periodic reading is performed, and trigger has no effect |
| Input | `trigger` | `BOOL` | External trigger signal, reads value upon each rising edge |
| Input | `value` | `LREAL` | Newly read data value |
| Input | `windowSize` | `INT` | The window size for the moving average, which must be between 1 and 100 |
| Input | `reset` | `BOOL` | Reset signal, resets the moving average calculation when TRUE |
| Output | `average` | `LREAL` | The moving average |
| Output | `windowSizeReached` | `BOOL` | FALSE: Maximum window width not yet reached TRUE: Maximum window width has been reached |
| Output | `error` | `BOOL` | FALSE: No error occurred TRUE: An error occurred during the execution of FB |
| Output | `status` | `WORD` | Status code |

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
cyclicExecutioninputBOOLWhen TRUE, periodic reading is performed, and trigger has no effect
triggerinputBOOLExternal trigger signal, reads value upon each rising edge
valueinputLREALNewly read data value
windowSizeinputINTThe window size for the moving average, which must be between 1 and 100
resetinputBOOLReset signal, resets the moving average calculation when TRUE
averageoutputLREALThe moving average
windowSizeReachedoutputBOOLFALSE: Maximum window width not yet reached TRUE: Maximum window width has been reached
erroroutputBOOLFALSE: No error occurred TRUE: An error occurred during the execution of FB
statusoutputWORDStatus code

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1窗口尺寸小于 1 或大于 100 时必须置 error=TRUE。
G((windowSize < 1 | windowSize > 100) -> error)
safetybounded_checker
S2错误状态置位时状态码必须为 16#8200（33280）。
G(error -> status = 33280)
safetybounded_checker
S3无错误时状态码必须为 16#0000。
G(!error -> status = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S4复位信号为 TRUE 时，移动平均必须清零。
G(reset -> average = 0.0)
safetybounded_checker
S5复位信号为 TRUE 时，窗口未满标志必须清除。
G(reset -> !windowSizeReached)
safetybounded_checker
S6在窗口大小固定的有效采样序列中，取最近 min(有效样本数, windowSize) 个值计算算术平均。重复值逐个计入，已采样数达到窗口长度时置 windowSizeReached。测试覆盖固定窗口中的平均值、满窗和淘汰。课程原题未规定扩大窗口时是否追回已淘汰的样本。—datasimulation
S7窗口在 1..100 范围时必须正常。
G((windowSize >= 1 AND windowSize <= 100) -> NOT error)
safetybounded_checker
S8尺寸一且此拍有有效采样时必须宣告满窗。
G((NOT reset AND windowSize = 1 AND (cyclicExecution OR rising(trigger))) -> windowSizeReached)
safetybounded_checker

public-medium-04

Temperature Logic Control

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 37 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# Temperature Logic Control

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

Write a function block FB to implement a temperature control logic. The program should include the following functions:
1. Read the value of the temperature sensor through an analog input module.
2. Compare the read temperature value TemperatureSensorInput with the set temperature value SetTemperature.
3. If the actual temperature is lower than the set temperature, start the heater HeaterOutput.
4. If the actual temperature is higher than the set temperature, turn off the heater HeaterOutput.
5. If the heater works continuously for more than a certain period (for convenience, using 5s), it should trigger an overheating protection, automatically turn off the heater, and must wait for a certain period (for convenience, using 3s) before the overheating protection can be lifted.
6. Provide status indication, displaying the current temperature, set temperature, heater status, and whether overheating protection is triggered.
7. Provide temperature sensor fault judgment, if the read temperature value TemperatureSensorInput is less than 0 or greater than 100, it is considered a sensor fault, and the heater should be turned off.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `TemperatureSensorInput` | `REAL` | The input value of the temperature sensor, i.e., the actual temperature |
| Input | `SetTemperature` | `REAL` | The set temperature value |
| Output | `CurrentTemperature` | `REAL` | Display of the current temperature |
| Output | `SetTempDisplay` | `REAL` | Display of the set temperature |
| Output | `HeaterStatus` | `BOOL` | Display of the heater status |
| Output | `OverheatProtection` | `BOOL` | Display of the overheating protection status |
| Output | `HeaterOutput` | `BOOL` | The control output of the heater |

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
TemperatureSensorInputinputREALThe input value of the temperature sensor, i.e., the actual temperature
SetTemperatureinputREALThe set temperature value
CurrentTemperatureoutputREALDisplay of the current temperature
SetTempDisplayoutputREALDisplay of the set temperature
HeaterStatusoutputBOOLDisplay of the heater status
OverheatProtectionoutputBOOLDisplay of the overheating protection status
HeaterOutputoutputBOOLThe control output of the heater

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1传感器读数低于 0 或高于 100 时，加热输出必须为 FALSE。
G((TemperatureSensorInput < 0.0 | TemperatureSensorInput > 100.0) -> !HeaterOutput)
safetybounded_checker
S2过热保护有效期间，加热输出必须被强制关断。
G(OverheatProtection -> !HeaterOutput)
safetybounded_checker
S3实际温度高于设定值时关闭加热。
G(TemperatureSensorInput > SetTemperature -> NOT HeaterOutput)
safetybounded_checker
S4无故障、无过热保护且读数低于设定温度时，加热输出必须为 TRUE（过载触发保护的当拍除外）。
G((TemperatureSensorInput < SetTemperature & TemperatureSensorInput >= 0.0 & TemperatureSensorInput <= 100.0 & !OverheatProtection) -> HeaterOutput)
safetybounded_checker
S5过热保护冷却至少 3 秒，按外部任务时钟检查。—livenesssimulation
S6显示值跟随当拍温度和设定值，状态指示跟随实际加热输出。
G((CurrentTemperature = TemperatureSensorInput) AND (SetTempDisplay = SetTemperature) AND (HeaterStatus <-> HeaterOutput))
databounded_checker
S7连续加热超过 5 秒后触发保护，按外部任务时钟检查。—safetysimulation

功能用例

public-medium-05

Automated Production Line Control

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 26 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# Automated Production Line Control

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

A simple automated production line consists of three workstations: Workstation A, Workstation B, and Workstation C. The product needs to go through these three stations in sequence to be considered fully completed. There is a motor in the production line that is responsible for moving the product from one workstation to the next. The sequence of the line is from Workstation A to Workstation B, and then to Workstation C. Each workstation has a sensor (used to detect if the product has arrived) and a button (used to trigger the signal that the station's processing is complete).
Control requirements:
1. Mode Switching: The mode switch (Mode) is used to switch between manual and automatic modes of the production line.
2. Manual Mode: In manual mode, the forward rotation button (ForwardButton) controls the forward rotation of the motor, and the reverse rotation button (ReverseButton) controls the reverse rotation of the motor. Forward and reverse rotation operations need to be interlocked and cannot be performed simultaneously.
3. Automatic Mode:
   - When the product arrives at Workstation A, SensorA detects the product, and after the operator triggers the processing complete signal via ButtonA, the motor starts forward rotation to move the product to Workstation B.
   - When the product arrives at Workstation B, SensorB detects the product, the motor stops, and after the operator triggers the processing complete signal via ButtonB, the motor starts forward rotation to move the product to Workstation C.
   - When the product arrives at Workstation C, SensorC detects the product, the motor stops, and after the operator triggers the processing complete signal via ButtonC, the completion indicator light (CompletionLight) turns on, indicating that the product processing is complete. When the product is taken away, i.e., SensorC no longer detects the product, the completion indicator light (CompletionLight) turns off.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `Mode` | `BOOL` | Used to switch the production line between manual and automatic modes, 0=manual mode, 1=automatic mode |
| Input | `ForwardButton` | `BOOL` | Forward rotation button |
| Input | `ReverseButton` | `BOOL` | Reverse rotation button |
| Input | `SensorA` | `BOOL` | Sensor A |
| Input | `SensorB` | `BOOL` | Sensor B |
| Input | `SensorC` | `BOOL` | Sensor C |
| Input | `ButtonA` | `BOOL` | Button A |
| Input | `ButtonB` | `BOOL` | Button B |
| Input | `ButtonC` | `BOOL` | Button C |
| Output | `MotorForward` | `BOOL` | Motor forward rotation output |
| Output | `MotorReverse` | `BOOL` | Motor reverse rotation output |
| Output | `CompletionLight` | `BOOL` | Indicator light for product processing completion |

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
ModeinputBOOLUsed to switch the production line between manual and automatic modes, 0=manual mode, 1=automatic mode
ForwardButtoninputBOOLForward rotation button
ReverseButtoninputBOOLReverse rotation button
SensorAinputBOOLSensor A
SensorBinputBOOLSensor B
SensorCinputBOOLSensor C
ButtonAinputBOOLButton A
ButtonBinputBOOLButton B
ButtonCinputBOOLButton C
MotorForwardoutputBOOLMotor forward rotation output
MotorReverseoutputBOOLMotor reverse rotation output
CompletionLightoutputBOOLIndicator light for product processing completion

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1正转与反转输出互锁，任何周期都不得同时为 TRUE。
G(!(MotorForward & MotorReverse))
safetybounded_checker
S2正转只可能出现在自动模式或手动按下正转按钮时。
G(MotorForward -> (Mode | ForwardButton))
safetybounded_checker
S3反转只在手动模式且按下反转按钮时输出。
G(MotorReverse -> (!Mode & ReverseButton))
safetybounded_checker
S4完成灯亮的前提是自动模式且 C 工位传感器检测到产品。
G(CompletionLight -> (SensorC & Mode))
safetybounded_checker
S5产品被取走后（下一周期 SensorC 不再检测），完成灯必须在下一周期熄灭。
G(CompletionLight & X(!SensorC) -> X(!CompletionLight))
sequencingbounded_checker
S6自动模式按 A->B->C 顺序推进：A 工位检测到产品且 ButtonA 后正转，B/C 传感器检测到即停，ButtonB 后继续正转，C 工位 ButtonC 后完成灯亮、产品取走即灭；手动模式下正转/反转按钮互锁，两者同时按下都不动作，切到手动会清空自动流程。—datasimulation
S7手动模式恰有一个方向按钮按下时，电机必须按该方向运行。
G(NOT Mode -> (((ForwardButton AND NOT ReverseButton) -> MotorForward) AND ((ReverseButton AND NOT ForwardButton) -> MotorReverse)))
safetybounded_checker
S8上电或从手动进入自动，A 工位有料且确认后开始向 B 输送。
G((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorA AND ButtonA) -> MotorForward)
safetybounded_checker
S9从自动开始经 A 确认、B 到位/确认、C 到位/确认的连续五拍应点亮完成灯。
G(((Mode AND SensorC AND ButtonC) AND prev(((Mode AND SensorC) AND prev(((Mode AND SensorB AND ButtonB) AND prev(((Mode AND SensorB) AND prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorA AND ButtonA))))))))) -> CompletionLight)
safetybounded_checker
S10C 工位仍有已完成产品且保持自动时，灯持续点亮。
G((Mode AND SensorC AND prev(CompletionLight)) -> CompletionLight)
safetybounded_checker
S11自动方式发出新的正转命令时，必须有 A 到位并确认，或 B 到位并确认。
G((Mode AND rising(MotorForward)) -> ((SensorA AND ButtonA) OR (SensorB AND ButtonB)))
safetybounded_checker
S12仍在自动输送且下游 B、C 都未到位时，已运行的正转电机必须保持，不得只在启动拍发一个脉冲。
G((Mode AND prev(Mode AND MotorForward) AND NOT SensorB AND NOT SensorC) -> MotorForward)
safetybounded_checker

public-medium-06

Slab Thickness Alarm and Fault History

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 23 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# Slab Thickness Alarm and Fault History

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

Monitor slab-thickness faults on a stone-cutting line, including minimum alarm holding, fault counting, normal-cutting runtime accumulation and a history of the five most recent faults.

1. When THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID is TRUE, THICKNESS_FAULT indicates that thickness deviation exceeds tolerance. Activate THICKNESS_ALARM immediately when the fault becomes TRUE, and hold each triggered alarm for at least 500 ms. Keep the alarm active while the fault persists.
2. On each FALSE-to-TRUE transition of a valid fault signal, increment Slab_Fault_Counter and store the current FaultTimestamp in element 0 of Slab_Fault_History. Shift older entries toward higher indices, retaining only the five most recent entries.
3. Accumulate Slab_Runtime_Accumulator using CycleTime only while CUTTING_RUN is TRUE, the thickness signal is valid and no thickness fault is present. Otherwise preserve the accumulated value.
4. At power-up or whenever THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID is FALSE, switch off the alarm and clear its holding state. Do not add fault records or accumulate normal runtime while the signal is invalid.
5. At power-up, initialize the fault count, accumulated runtime and all five history entries to zero. Statistics and history are for observation and maintenance only; they must not alter the cutting equipment's operating permissive, and external callers must not modify history entries.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `THICKNESS_FAULT` | `BOOL` | Thickness deviation exceeds the permitted tolerance. |
| Input | `THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID` | `BOOL` | Thickness-signal validity; FALSE means the signal is lost or unavailable. |
| Input | `CUTTING_RUN` | `BOOL` | Actual cutting-equipment running status. |
| Input | `FaultTimestamp` | `DINT` | Current timestamp in seconds, used as the fault-record identifier. |
| Input | `CycleTime` | `TIME` | Actual elapsed interval represented by this invocation; must be positive. |
| Output | `THICKNESS_ALARM` | `BOOL` | Thickness alarm with a minimum 500 ms hold duration. |
| Output | `Slab_Fault_Counter` | `UINT` | Number of valid thickness-fault occurrences. |
| Output | `Slab_Runtime_Accumulator` | `LTIME` | Accumulated normal-cutting runtime. |
| Output | `Slab_Fault_History` | `ARRAY[0..4] OF DINT` | Five most recent fault timestamps; index 0 is newest; read-only to callers. |

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
THICKNESS_FAULTinputBOOLThickness deviation exceeds the permitted tolerance.
THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALIDinputBOOLThickness-signal validity; FALSE means the signal is lost or unavailable.
CUTTING_RUNinputBOOLActual cutting-equipment running status.
FaultTimestampinputDINTCurrent timestamp in seconds, used as the fault-record identifier.
CycleTimeinputTIMEActual elapsed interval represented by this invocation; must be positive.
THICKNESS_ALARMoutputBOOLThickness alarm with a minimum 500 ms hold duration.
Slab_Fault_CounteroutputUINTNumber of valid thickness-fault occurrences.
Slab_Runtime_AccumulatoroutputLTIMEAccumulated normal-cutting runtime.
Slab_Fault_HistoryoutputARRAY[0..4] OF DINTFive most recent fault timestamps; index 0 is newest; read-only to callers.

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1当厚度信号无效时，厚度报警必须为 FALSE（无效信号不得报警）
G(THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID = FALSE -> THICKNESS_ALARM = FALSE)
safetytimed_simulation
S2有效健康切割的运行时间按实际调用间隔递增，TIME以毫秒、LTIME以纳秒记录。
G((CUTTING_RUN AND THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID AND NOT THICKNESS_FAULT) -> Slab_Runtime_Accumulator = prev(Slab_Runtime_Accumulator) + CycleTime * 1000000)
datatimed_simulation
S3当信号有效且厚度故障存在时，厚度报警必须为 TRUE（有效故障立即报警）
G(THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID = TRUE AND THICKNESS_FAULT = TRUE -> THICKNESS_ALARM = TRUE)
sequencingtimed_simulation
S4可表示时段内，运行时间只增或保持。
G(Slab_Runtime_Accumulator >= prev(Slab_Runtime_Accumulator))
safetytimed_simulation
S5历史 index0 记录到时间戳时，故障计数必须已经至少为 1
G(Slab_Fault_History[0] <> 0 -> Slab_Fault_Counter >= 1)
datatimed_simulation
S6故障计数与 5 条历史队列的写入与移位顺序（未满 5 条与满后移位两种状态）：由用例验证的数据性质—datatimed_simulation
S7无合格切割时运行累计保持。
G(NOT (CUTTING_RUN AND THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID AND NOT THICKNESS_FAULT) -> Slab_Runtime_Accumulator = prev(Slab_Runtime_Accumulator))
safetytimed_simulation
S8仅有效故障的上升沿累加计数，在未达到 UINT 上限时加一。
G(((rising((THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID AND THICKNESS_FAULT)) AND NOT prev(Slab_Fault_Counter = 65535)) -> Slab_Fault_Counter = prev(Slab_Fault_Counter) + 1) AND (NOT rising((THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID AND THICKNESS_FAULT)) -> Slab_Fault_Counter = prev(Slab_Fault_Counter)))
safetytimed_simulation
S9新故障写入时间戳并逐槽后移其余四条，无新沿时全部五条保持。
G((rising((THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID AND THICKNESS_FAULT)) -> (Slab_Fault_History[0] = FaultTimestamp AND Slab_Fault_History[1] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[0]) AND Slab_Fault_History[2] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[1]) AND Slab_Fault_History[3] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[2]) AND Slab_Fault_History[4] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[3]))) AND (NOT rising((THICKNESS_SIGNAL_VALID AND THICKNESS_FAULT)) -> (Slab_Fault_History[0] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[0]) AND Slab_Fault_History[1] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[1]) AND Slab_Fault_History[2] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[2]) AND Slab_Fault_History[3] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[3]) AND Slab_Fault_History[4] = prev(Slab_Fault_History[4]))))
safetytimed_simulation
S10报警至少保持到触发后500毫秒，故障持续时保持；信号无效时立即清除。按实际间隔及触发后499毫秒的边界检查。—safetytimed_simulation

public-medium-07

Office HVAC Multi-mode Energy-Saving Control

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 20 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# Office HVAC Multi-mode Energy-Saving Control

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

Control an office-building supply fan and generate a temperature-setpoint adjustment from occupancy, operating mode and the energy-saving selection.

1. Valid HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR values are 0 (Auto), 1 (Comfort), 2 (Economy) and 3 (Night).
2. Start a 30-second delay only when occupancy is detected and Night mode is not selected. SUPPLY_FAN_RUN becomes TRUE only after these conditions remain satisfied for 30 seconds. Immediately stop the fan and reset timing if occupancy is lost or Night mode is selected. Wait the full 30 seconds again when the conditions recover.
3. Auto mode: use a 0°C adjustment when occupied. When unoccupied, use +3°C in energy-saving mode or +2°C in standard mode.
4. Comfort mode: use 0°C when occupied or +1°C when unoccupied. Economy mode: use 0°C when occupied or +4°C when unoccupied.
5. Night mode: use +4°C regardless of occupancy. The energy-saving selection affects only Auto mode.
6. Keep TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST within ±4°C. Initialize outputs to OFF and zero at power-up; update them from current inputs when normal calculation begins.
7. The start delay controls only the BOOL fan enable; no continuous speed ramp is required.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `OCCUPANCY_DETECTED` | `BOOL` | Occupancy-detection result derived from the CO₂ sensor. |
| Input | `ENERGY_SAVING_MODE` | `BOOL` | TRUE selects energy saving; FALSE selects standard operation; affects Auto mode only. |
| Input | `HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR` | `INT` | 0 Auto, 1 Comfort, 2 Economy, 3 Night; callers supply a valid value. |
| Output | `SUPPLY_FAN_RUN` | `BOOL` | Supply-fan run enable. |
| Output | `TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST` | `INT` | Temperature-setpoint adjustment in °C. |

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
OCCUPANCY_DETECTEDinputBOOLOccupancy-detection result derived from the CO₂ sensor.
ENERGY_SAVING_MODEinputBOOLTRUE selects energy saving; FALSE selects standard operation; affects Auto mode only.
HVAC_MODE_SELECTORinputINT0 Auto, 1 Comfort, 2 Economy, 3 Night; callers supply a valid value.
SUPPLY_FAN_RUNoutputBOOLSupply-fan run enable.
TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUSToutputINTTemperature-setpoint adjustment in °C.

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1当送风机运行时，HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR 必须不是 Night(3)
G(SUPPLY_FAN_RUN = TRUE -> HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR <> 3)
safetybounded_checker
S2当送风机运行时，必须处于占用状态
G(SUPPLY_FAN_RUN = TRUE -> OCCUPANCY_DETECTED = TRUE)
safetybounded_checker
S3温度设定值修正必须始终落在 ±4 ℃ 之内
G(TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST >= -4 AND TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST <= 4)
safetybounded_checker
S4当选择 Night(3) 时，温度设定值修正必须为 +4 ℃
G(HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR = 3 -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 4)
sequencingbounded_checker
S5当选择 Auto(0) 且检测到占用时，温度设定值修正必须为 0
G(HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR = 0 AND OCCUPANCY_DETECTED = TRUE -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 0)
sequencingbounded_checker
S6四种有效模式的温度调整由功能用例核对。—datasimulation
S7在有效的非夜间模式下，有人时调节值为0；无人时由模式和节能选择精确决定。
G(((OCCUPANCY_DETECTED AND HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR >= 0 AND HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR <= 2) -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 0) AND ((NOT OCCUPANCY_DETECTED AND HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR = 0 AND ENERGY_SAVING_MODE) -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 3) AND ((NOT OCCUPANCY_DETECTED AND HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR = 0 AND NOT ENERGY_SAVING_MODE) -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 2) AND ((NOT OCCUPANCY_DETECTED AND HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR = 1) -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 1) AND ((NOT OCCUPANCY_DETECTED AND HVAC_MODE_SELECTOR = 2) -> TEMP_SETPOINT_ADJUST = 4))
safetybounded_checker
S8持续占用且非夜间满 30 秒才启机，中断后重新计时。—safetysimulation

功能用例

public-medium-08

IPv4 Parsing with Format and Range Checks

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 14 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

课程原文（含接口）

# IPv4 Parsing with Format and Range Checks

- **Difficulty:** Medium (medium)

## Requirements

Design a PLC function block in Structured Text (ST) that parses a dotted-decimal IPv4 string into a 32-bit unsigned integer.

1. ipText must contain four nonempty decimal-digit segments separated by periods. Each segment must have a value from 0 through 255.
2. Assemble the address in network byte order: the first segment occupies the most significant 8 bits and the fourth occupies the least significant 8 bits. Write the result to ipValue.
3. Set ipValue to 0 for malformed input, a nondigit segment, an incorrect segment count or any out-of-range segment. The valid address 0.0.0.0 also produces 0.

## Interface Definition

| Direction | Variable/Field | Data Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `ipText` | `STRING(254)` | IPv4 text to parse, with a capacity of 254 characters. A valid dotted-decimal address requires at most 15 characters. |
| Output | `ipValue` | `UDINT` | 32-bit address in network byte order; zero for invalid input. |

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
ipTextinputSTRING(254)IPv4 text to parse, with a capacity of 254 characters. A valid dotted-decimal address requires at most 15 characters.
ipValueoutputUDINT32-bit address in network byte order; zero for invalid input.

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1非法 IPv4 输入必须输出 0，合法性由公开输入计算。
G(__spec_ip_scored -> (NOT __spec_ip_valid -> ipValue = 0))
safetybounded_checker
S2合法四段 IPv4 按网络字节序输出；不约束候选内部计数器。
G(__spec_ip_scored -> (__spec_ip_valid -> ipValue = __spec_ip_value))
databounded_checker
S3只有四段全 255 的合法输入才允许输出最大地址。
G(__spec_ip_scored -> (ipValue = 4294967295 -> __spec_ip_valid AND __spec_ip_value = 4294967295))
sequencingbounded_checker
S4在课程题面确定的样例中检查格式、范围和网络字节序。—datasimulation

self-easy-01

分段采样平均值计算中的除零与边界保护

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 18 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，在每个扫描周期计算一组采样值的平均值。 控制要求： 1. enable 为 TRUE 时更新平均值，为 FALSE 时 avgOut 保持上一周期的值不变； 2. count 为本周期有效样本个数，合法范围 0..8；count 为 0 时**不得发生除零**，此时 avgOut 保持不变； 3. count 大于数组长度 8 时，只按前 8 个样本计算，不得越界访问； 4. avgEnable 为 FALSE 时视为未投入自动，同样保持 avgOut 不变； 5. 整数相加可能超过 INT 范围，求和过程中应使用更宽的类型（DINT/LINT）避免溢出； 6. 结果以 REAL 输出，保留原始精度，不做四舍五入到整数。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
enableinputBOOLTRUE 时更新平均值，FALSE 时保持
valuesinputARRAY[1..8] OF INT本周期采集到的原始样本（1..8 号站）
countinputINT本周期有效的样本个数，合法范围 0..8
avgEnableinputBOOL计算使能，通常接在物位低联锁之后
avgOutoutputREAL平均输出

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1count 为 0 或为负时，不得发生除零，avgOut 应在**当周期**保持不变。
G((count <= 0) -> avgOut = prev(avgOut))
safetybounded_checker
S2enable 为 FALSE 时，avgOut 应在当周期保持不变，不得被改写。
G(!enable -> avgOut = prev(avgOut))
safetybounded_checker
S3avgOut 在任何输入下都不是 NaN（数值健全性）。越界访问本身由解释器直接判运行期错误，不在 LTL 层表达。
G(avgOut = avgOut)
safetybounded_checker
S4在 enable 且 count 合法时，avgOut 等于所选样本的算术平均。—datasimulation
S5自动平均未投入时保留上拍输出。
G(!avgEnable -> avgOut = prev(avgOut))
safetybounded_checker
S6投入且样本数为正，按实际有效前缀的宽类型和除以裁剪后的长度。
G((enable AND avgEnable) -> (((count = 1) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1])) / 1.0) AND ((count = 2) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2])) / 2.0) AND ((count = 3) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[3])) / 3.0) AND ((count = 4) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[3]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[4])) / 4.0) AND ((count = 5) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[3]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[4]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[5])) / 5.0) AND ((count = 6) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[3]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[4]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[5]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[6])) / 6.0) AND ((count = 7) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[3]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[4]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[5]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[6]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[7])) / 7.0) AND ((count >= 8) -> avgOut = (INT_TO_REAL(values[1]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[2]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[3]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[4]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[5]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[6]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[7]) + INT_TO_REAL(values[8])) / 8.0)))
safetybounded_checker

功能用例

self-easy-02

储罐高低液位联锁与报警自保持

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 20 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

实现储罐进料联锁功能块 FB。每拍采样液位、进料许可和复位按钮；所有输出在同一拍结束时判定。初始两个报警和上一拍按钮均为 FALSE。 1. tankLevel >= 85.0 是高液位危险条件，tankLevel <= 15.0 是低液位危险条件。每个报警在各自条件成立时立即置位并锁存。回到正常区本身不清除报警。 2. resetBtn 从 FALSE 变为 TRUE 的那一拍，分别检查每个报警：危险条件已消失才清除，条件仍成立则保持置位。两项均可同时锁存；复位只能清除已安全的那一项。危险条件刚出现的同一拍按下复位，危险条件仍优先。 3. 按住复位跨越液位变化不产生新复位；需先松手再按。首次扫描前按钮按 FALSE 处理。 4. 本拍更新报警后，只有 inletPermit 为 TRUE 且两个报警均为 FALSE 才运行进料泵 pumpRun。出口阀 outletValveOpen 在低报警锁存期间关闭，其他情况下开启。outletFlow 是预留观测接口，本题不按流量设动作阈值。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
tankLevelinputREAL储罐液位百分比 0..100
outletFlowinputREAL出口流量 m³/h
inletPermitinputBOOL进料许可（工艺联锁允许进料）
resetBtninputBOOL复位按钮（上升沿有效）
pumpRunoutputBOOL进料泵运行命令
alarmHighoutputBOOL高液位报警（自保持，需复位）
alarmLowoutputBOOL低液位报警（自保持，需复位）
outletValveOpenoutputBOOL出口阀开命令

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1高液位危险与复位同拍也必须报警，危险条件优先。
G(tankLevel >= 85.0 -> alarmHigh)
safetybounded_checker
S2高液位报警置位后，在复位按钮上升沿出现之前必须保持。
G(alarmHigh & !X(rising(resetBtn)) -> X(alarmHigh))
sequencingbounded_checker
S3高液位报警有效期间，进料泵不得运行（停止优先于启动）。
G(alarmHigh -> !pumpRun)
safetybounded_checker
S4液位不高于 15.0 时，出口阀必须在当周期关闭。
G(tankLevel <= 15.0 -> !outletValveOpen)
safetybounded_checker
S5无进料许可时，进料泵不得运行。
G(!inletPermit -> !pumpRun)
safetybounded_checker
N10-S211.10补充：已脱离高阈值时，高报警等于上拍锁存且本拍没有复位沿。
G(tankLevel < 85.0 -> (alarmHigh <-> (prev(alarmHigh) AND !rising(resetBtn))))
sequencingtest_trace_audit
N10-S311.10补充：高或低报警任一有效，当拍禁止运行进料泵。
G((alarmHigh OR alarmLow) -> !pumpRun)
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S411.10补充：阀开命令与低报警互反，包括复位拍。
G(outletValveOpen <-> !alarmLow)
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S511.10补充：两报警均清除时，进料泵命令等于本拍进料许可。
G((!alarmHigh AND !alarmLow) -> (pumpRun <-> inletPermit))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S611.10补充：达到或低于低阈值即置位低报警；危险消失前复位无效，之后需新的复位沿清除。
G(alarmLow <-> ((tankLevel <= 15.0) OR (prev(alarmLow) AND !rising(resetBtn))))
sequencingtest_trace_audit

功能用例

全部输出首次达到低阈值与复位上升沿同拍；高报警可清除

self-hard-01

带确认与优先级的报警管理（四态报警模型）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 15 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，实现 ISA-18.2 风格的四态报警管理。 控制要求： 1. 报警条件成立（flowLow = TRUE）→ alarmActive 置 TRUE，alarmAcked 置 FALSE； 2. 报警未被确认时，即使条件消失，alarmActive 仍保持 TRUE（报警在未确认前不得消失），同时 alarmAcked 置 TRUE 表示'已转为待确认清除'； 3. ackBtn 的**上升沿**确认报警：若此时条件已消失 → alarmActive 与 alarmAcked 一起清除；若条件仍存在 → 只置 alarmAcked，报警继续； 4. sensorOK 为 FALSE 表示传感器断线 → highHigh 置 TRUE（高优先级）；**高优先级报警与低优先级报警必须同时可见，不得互相抑制**； 5. 传感器恢复后，highHigh 自动清除，低优先级仍按 1~3 的规则独立判定。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
flowLowinputBOOL流量低开关量输入
ackBtninputBOOL确认按钮（上升沿有效）
sensorOKinputBOOL传感器健康状态（FALSE 表示断线）
alarmActiveoutputBOOL报警激活（条件仍存在）
alarmAckedoutputBOOL报警已确认（条件消失后自动清除）
highHighoutputBOOL高优先级报警（传感器断线导致）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1报警条件持续存在时，确认按钮不得使报警消失（当周期即成立）。
G(flowLow & ackBtn -> alarmActive)
safetybounded_checker
S2未经确认的报警不会自行消失：条件仍在且未确认时，下一拍必须仍激活。
G(alarmActive & !alarmAcked & X(flowLow) -> X(alarmActive))
safetybounded_checker
S3传感器断线时高优先级报警必须在当周期置位。
G(!sensorOK -> highHigh)
safetybounded_checker
S4确认只在上升沿生效：确认沿出现且报警条件仍在时，当拍即置已确认。
G(rising(ackBtn) & flowLow -> alarmAcked)
sequencingbounded_checker
S5断线高报警与传感器状态双向一致，恢复同拍须清除。
G(highHigh <-> !sensorOK)
safetybounded_checker
S6低报警出现即有效；条件消失后仅实际确认或新确认沿允许清除。
G(alarmActive <-> (flowLow OR (prev(alarmActive) AND !rising(ackBtn) AND !prev(flowLow AND alarmAcked))))
safetybounded_checker
S7区分低报警持续时的真实确认记忆与条件消失后的待清标志。
G(alarmAcked <-> (alarmActive AND (!flowLow OR rising(ackBtn) OR prev(flowLow AND alarmActive AND alarmAcked))))
safetybounded_checker

功能用例

self-hard-02

批次工艺阶段与运行时长统计（无外部时钟）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 47 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，管理间歇反应釜批次。初始 (phase, elapsed, timeout)=(0,0,FALSE)。 1. maxDuration 为正整数，单位为扫描拍；所有实数输入为有限值，不使用系统时钟或计时器。 2. 每拍先记录 startBtn 上升沿，包括中止当拍；按 中止 > 新启动沿 > 运行计数 的优先级处理。 3. abortBtn 为 TRUE 时立即进入 phase=9，elapsed 与 timeout 保持；同拍启动沿已消耗，重新启动必须松开再按。 4. 无中止且出现新启动沿时进入 phase=1，elapsed=0、timeout=FALSE；启动拍不计数。 5. 上一拍 phase 为1或2时先将 elapsed 加1。达到本拍 maxDuration 则 timeout=TRUE、phase=3，优先于升温达标；否则 phase=1 且 pv>=setpoint 时进入 phase=2。 6. phase=3 为完成态、phase=9 为中止态，两态冻结计数及超时标志，直到新的有效启动或中止。不设无接口支持的操作员确认。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
startBtninputBOOL批次开始（上升沿有效）
abortBtninputBOOL中止按钮（直接中止批次）
setpointinputREAL工艺设定值
pvinputREAL过程测量值
maxDurationinputINT本拍超时门槛，正整数扫描拍数
elapsedoutputINT本批次已计入的扫描拍数，启动拍为0
phaseoutputINT阶段号：0=空闲 1=升温 2=保温 3=完成 9=已中止
timeoutoutputBOOL超时标志

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1无中止、无新启动沿且上一拍处于运行态时，恰好计数一拍（含完成拍）。
G((prev(TRUE) AND prev(phase=1 OR phase=2) AND NOT abortBtn AND NOT (startBtn AND NOT prev(startBtn))) -> elapsed=prev(elapsed)+1)
sequencingbounded_checker
S2运行计数达到本拍正时限时进入超时完成态3。
G((prev(TRUE) AND prev(phase=1 OR phase=2) AND NOT abortBtn AND NOT (startBtn AND NOT prev(startBtn)) AND maxDuration<=prev(elapsed)+1) -> (timeout AND phase=3))
safetybounded_checker
S3中止优先于同拍启动，并冻结计数与超时标志。
G(abortBtn -> (phase=9 AND elapsed=prev(elapsed) AND timeout=prev(timeout)))
safetybounded_checker
S4完成和中止态在无新启动且无中止时保持。
G((prev(TRUE) AND prev(phase=3 OR phase=9) AND NOT abortBtn AND NOT (startBtn AND NOT prev(startBtn))) -> (phase=prev(phase) AND elapsed=prev(elapsed) AND timeout=prev(timeout)))
safetybounded_checker
S5仅未中止的新启动沿开始批次并清零，启动拍不计数。
G(((startBtn AND NOT prev(startBtn)) AND NOT abortBtn) -> (elapsed=0 AND NOT timeout AND phase=1))
sequencingbounded_checker
N10-S111.10补充：本拍未中止且无启动沿时，若上一拍为运行态则累计拍数恰增 1，否则拍数保持；启动和中止分别按 S5、S3 处理。
G((!abortBtn AND !rising(startBtn)) -> ((prev((phase = 1) OR (phase = 2)) -> elapsed = prev(elapsed) + 1) AND (!prev((phase = 1) OR (phase = 2)) -> elapsed = prev(elapsed))))
sequencingtest_trace_audit
N10-S211.10补充：本拍未中止且无新启动沿：上一拍为运行态时，以本拍累计拍数和门槛判超时，达到或超过即置位并进入完成态；上一拍不在运行态时保持超时标志。
G((!abortBtn AND !rising(startBtn)) -> ((prev((phase = 1) OR (phase = 2)) -> ((timeout <-> (elapsed >= maxDuration)) AND (timeout -> phase = 3))) AND (!prev((phase = 1) OR (phase = 2)) -> (timeout <-> prev(timeout)))))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S311.10补充：中止命令有效即进入中止态，并保持上一拍的累计拍数及超时标志；与启动上升沿同拍仍以中止优先。
G(abortBtn -> (phase = 9 AND elapsed = prev(elapsed) AND (timeout <-> prev(timeout))))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S411.10补充：无中止、无新启动沿时，上一拍非运行态保持原阶段。空闲保持 0，完成保持 3，中止保持 9；完成后若收到中止命令按 S3 转到 9，重新启动按 S5 处理。
G((!abortBtn AND !rising(startBtn) AND !prev((phase = 1) OR (phase = 2))) -> phase = prev(phase))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S511.10补充：没有中止命令时，启动上升沿开始新批次并清零计数及超时标志；中止优先。
G((rising(startBtn) AND NOT abortBtn) -> (elapsed = 0 AND NOT timeout AND phase = 1))
sequencingtest_trace_audit
N10-S611.10补充：运行计数拍若尚未超时，升温阶段在 pv 达到或超过设定值时进入保温，否则继续升温；保温阶段始终维持到超时、中止或新启动。
G((!abortBtn AND !rising(startBtn) AND prev((phase = 1) OR (phase = 2)) AND elapsed < maxDuration) -> (((prev(phase = 2) OR pv >= setpoint) -> phase = 2) AND ((prev(phase = 1) AND pv < setpoint) -> phase = 1)))
sequencingtest_trace_audit

功能用例

self-hard-03

固定容量物料 FIFO（紧凑对外数组、满空与同拍出入）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 45 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

功能块 FB 管理容量为 8 的物料队列，输入按钮由低到高仅触发一次；持续按住不重复执行。初始 count=0、队列为空，两个按钮的初始历史均为 FALSE。 1. popEn 的上升沿先执行出队：非空则移除第一项，空队列不变。 2. pushEn 的上升沿随后执行入队：若此时 count<8，将 pushItem 追加到逻辑队尾；否则拒绝且原队列不变。满队列同时出入时先腾出一个槽再追加；空队列同时出入时只留下新物料。 3. 输出 count 为当前长度，full 当且仅当 count=8，empty 当且仅当 count=0。空队列 head=0，非空时 head 是第一项物料号。 4. fifo 为调用方绑定、运行期间不被调用方改写的可读写数组。每拍结束时 fifo[1..count] 是按出队次序紧凑排列的逻辑队列，fifo[1] 在非空时等于 head。非有效区间 fifo[count+1..8] 的值未规定，不得据此判错。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
pushEninputBOOL入队指令（上升沿有效）
pushIteminputINT入队物料号
popEninputBOOL出队指令（上升沿有效）
fifoinoutARRAY[1..8] OF INT队列缓冲区，下标 1 为最老元素
countoutputINT当前队列元素个数 0..8
fulloutputBOOL队列满
emptyoutputBOOL队列空
headoutputINT队首元素（空时保持 0）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1长度属于 0..8，空、满标志与长度双向一致。
G((count >= 0 AND count <= 8) AND (full <-> count = 8) AND (empty <-> count = 0))
safetybounded_checker
S2上一拍已满，本拍只有入队上升沿时保持长度。
G(prev(full) AND rising(pushEn) AND !rising(popEn) -> count = 8)
safetybounded_checker
S3上一拍为空，本拍只有出队上升沿时保持长度为零。
G(prev(empty) AND rising(popEn) AND !rising(pushEn) -> count = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S4每拍结束后按出队顺序检查全部有效前缀；满队时逐槽比较，非满时只断言 fifo[1..count]。—datasimulation
S5空队列的 head 为零；非空时 head 就是可见有效前缀的第一项。
G((empty -> head = 0) AND (NOT empty -> head = fifo[1]))
sequencingbounded_checker
S6按先出后入计算本拍长度；仅成功的单边动作改变计数。
G((((rising(pushEn) AND !rising(popEn) AND !prev(count >= 8)) OR (rising(pushEn) AND rising(popEn) AND !prev(count <> 0))) -> count = prev(count) + 1) AND ((!rising(pushEn) AND rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) -> count = prev(count) - 1) AND ((!((rising(pushEn) AND !rising(popEn) AND !prev(count >= 8)) OR (rising(pushEn) AND rising(popEn) AND !prev(count <> 0)) OR (!rising(pushEn) AND rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)))) -> count = prev(count)))
safetybounded_checker
S7接受入队时，新增逻辑队尾等于本拍物料号。
G((rising(pushEn) AND (rising(popEn) OR !prev(count >= 8)) AND count >= 1 AND count <= 8) -> fifo[count] = pushItem)
safetybounded_checker
S8逐槽校验有效前缀；成功出队才左移一位，其余原有有效元素保持。
G(((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 1)) -> fifo[1] = prev(fifo[1])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 1)) -> fifo[1] = prev(fifo[2])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 2)) -> fifo[2] = prev(fifo[2])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 2)) -> fifo[2] = prev(fifo[3])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 3)) -> fifo[3] = prev(fifo[3])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 3)) -> fifo[3] = prev(fifo[4])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 4)) -> fifo[4] = prev(fifo[4])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 4)) -> fifo[4] = prev(fifo[5])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 5)) -> fifo[5] = prev(fifo[5])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 5)) -> fifo[5] = prev(fifo[6])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 6)) -> fifo[6] = prev(fifo[6])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 6)) -> fifo[6] = prev(fifo[7])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 7)) -> fifo[7] = prev(fifo[7])) AND (((rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count > 7)) -> fifo[7] = prev(fifo[8])) AND ((!(rising(popEn) AND prev(count > 0)) AND prev(count >= 8)) -> fifo[8] = prev(fifo[8])))
safetybounded_checker

self-hard-04

多泵均衡运行与主备切换（数组遍历与迟滞阈值）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 32 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，管理 5 台并联泵的均衡运行。 1. demand 为工艺需求台数，**超出 0..5 时先按边界裁剪**（负数按 0、大于 5 按 5）；runningCount 输出实际运行台数，必须严格等于裁剪后的需求； 2. runHours[1..5] 是各泵累计运行小时（非负整数）；全场按（工时，泵号）升序比较，工时相同取小泵号； 3. leadPump 为主泵号、lagPump 为备用泵号，需求至少 2 台时两者必须是不同的泵；需求 0 台时两者都为 0；需求 1 台时 lagPump = 0； 4. **主泵迟滞优先于「选最短工时」**： - 需求由 0 变为正数（重新启动）时按当前工时初选主泵； - 正需求期间，rotated 是维护逻辑下发的**轮换请求（按电平逐拍读，不是边沿）**：只有请求有效、且**当前主泵的运行小时数比全场最短工时泵多严格超过 50 小时**时，才换主为那台最短工时泵；否则即使收到请求也保持原主泵；无请求时永不换主； - 需求归零时清空主备（leadPump = lagPump = 0），下次正需求重新初选； 5. **备泵选择**：需求 ≥ 2 时，只在「初选、需求台数上升、换主」这三种时刻从其余泵里选（工时，泵号）最小者作备泵；其余拍保留原备泵，不要每拍重选； 6. 数组下标必须按题面 1..5 访问，不得把 0 号槽用掉。 比较需求台数是否上升时，使用裁剪后的本拍与上一拍台数。实例初始 leadPump、lagPump、runningCount 均为 0。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
demandinputINT需求泵台数 0..5（超出请先按边界裁剪）
runHoursinputARRAY[1..5] OF LINT各泵累计运行小时数（只读）
leadPumpoutputINT当前主泵号 1..5，0 表示尚未选定
lagPumpoutputINT当前备用泵号 1..5，0 表示无
runningCountoutputINT实际运行泵台数
rotatedinputBOOL本周期存在轮换请求（输入，由维护逻辑置位）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1运行台数严格等于裁剪后的需求：小于 0 取 0，大于 5 取 5，否则等于需求。
G(((demand < 0 -> runningCount = 0) AND (demand > 5 -> runningCount = 5) AND ((demand >= 0 AND demand <= 5) -> runningCount = demand)))
safetybounded_checker
S2正需求时主泵编号在 1..5；需求 1 台无备泵，需求至少 2 台时备泵编号在 1..5 且与主泵不同。
G(((demand > 0 -> (leadPump >= 1 AND leadPump <= 5)) AND (demand = 1 -> lagPump = 0) AND (demand >= 2 -> (lagPump >= 1 AND lagPump <= 5 AND leadPump <> lagPump))))
safetybounded_checker
S3原主泵有效且需求为正时，如主泵发生变更，原主泵在本拍的工时须比新主泵严格多于 50 小时。
G((prev(leadPump >= 1 AND leadPump <= 5) AND leadPump >= 1 AND leadPump <= 5 AND leadPump <> prev(leadPump) AND demand > 0) -> runHours[prev(leadPump)] - runHours[leadPump] > 50)
safetybounded_checker
S4需求为 0 时不得有任何泵处于选中状态（当周期即成立）。
G(demand <= 0 -> leadPump = 0 & lagPump = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S5需求为 0 时运行台数必须为 0。
G(demand <= 0 -> runningCount = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S6正需求期间，若上拍已有主泵且本拍没有轮换请求，主泵必须保持。
G((NOT rotated & prev((leadPump <> 0)) & demand > 0) -> leadPump = prev(leadPump))
safetybounded_checker
S7初选时选全场（工时，泵号）最小者；有主泵时，如果本拍有轮换请求且原主泵比全场最短工时严格多于 50 小时，本拍必须换成全场最小者。
G(((demand > 0 AND leadPump >= 1 AND leadPump <= 5 AND (NOT prev(leadPump <> 0) OR (rotated AND prev(leadPump >= 1 AND leadPump <= 5) AND runHours[prev(leadPump)] - MIN(runHours[1],runHours[2],runHours[3],runHours[4],runHours[5]) > 50))) -> ((runHours[leadPump] < runHours[1] OR (runHours[leadPump] = runHours[1] AND leadPump <= 1)) AND (runHours[leadPump] < runHours[2] OR (runHours[leadPump] = runHours[2] AND leadPump <= 2)) AND (runHours[leadPump] < runHours[3] OR (runHours[leadPump] = runHours[3] AND leadPump <= 3)) AND (runHours[leadPump] < runHours[4] OR (runHours[leadPump] = runHours[4] AND leadPump <= 4)) AND (runHours[leadPump] < runHours[5] OR (runHours[leadPump] = runHours[5] AND leadPump <= 5)))))
safetybounded_checker
S8需要备泵时，在初选、换主或实际运行台数上升的当拍，以新主泵为排除项，选择其余泵中（工时，泵号）最小者。
G(((demand >= 2 AND lagPump >= 1 AND lagPump <= 5 AND (leadPump <> prev(leadPump) OR runningCount > prev(runningCount))) -> ((leadPump = 1 OR (runHours[lagPump] < runHours[1] OR (runHours[lagPump] = runHours[1] AND lagPump <= 1))) AND (leadPump = 2 OR (runHours[lagPump] < runHours[2] OR (runHours[lagPump] = runHours[2] AND lagPump <= 2))) AND (leadPump = 3 OR (runHours[lagPump] < runHours[3] OR (runHours[lagPump] = runHours[3] AND lagPump <= 3))) AND (leadPump = 4 OR (runHours[lagPump] < runHours[4] OR (runHours[lagPump] = runHours[4] AND lagPump <= 4))) AND (leadPump = 5 OR (runHours[lagPump] < runHours[5] OR (runHours[lagPump] = runHours[5] AND lagPump <= 5))))))
safetybounded_checker
S9仍需备泵、主泵不变且裁剪后台数未上升时，保持上拍备泵。
G(((demand >= 2 AND leadPump = prev(leadPump) AND runningCount <= prev(runningCount)) -> lagPump = prev(lagPump)))
safetybounded_checker

self-hard-05

燃烧状态与氧量修正联锁（熄火跳闸 + 分析仪故障降级）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 25 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，实现燃烧器的火焰联锁与烟气氧量修正。 控制要求： 1. 运行请求 runRequest 为 TRUE 且火焰探测器 flameOK 为 FALSE 时，**当拍**置位熄火跳闸 tripLatched，并切断燃料许可； 2. tripLatched 自保持，只有在 runRequest=FALSE 且 flameOK=FALSE（停机且无火焰的安全态）的前提下，resetBtn 的**上升沿**才能清除。复位是边沿命令而不是保持命令：按住的时长不影响判定，每拍只认有没有新的上升沿；复位那一刻条件不满足就无效，之后条件满足了但还按着（没有新上升沿）也**不得**清除；runRequest 仍为 TRUE 或 flameOK 仍为 TRUE 时按下的复位同样无效； 3. fuelPermit = runRequest AND flameOK AND NOT tripLatched； 4. 氧量修正：trimEnable = fuelPermit AND NOT o2Fault；airTrim = 2.0 * (2.0 - o2PV)，并钳位到 [-10, +10]；trimEnable=FALSE 时 airTrim 必须为 0.0。说明：±10 是**输出约束**，不代表两个方向都能到饱和——合法量程 0..21% 内原始修正只在 [-38, +4] 之间（o2PV=0 得 +4，o2PV=21 得 -38），因此正向限幅在本题永远不触发，只有 o2PV > 7.0 时负向限幅才起作用；增益偏小属于演示取舍，真实装置的风门增益取决于风道流通能力； 5. o2Fault = NOT o2Valid OR o2PV < 0.0 OR o2PV > 21.0（分析仪故障只切除修正，**不得**切除燃料；o2Valid=TRUE 但 o2PV<0 同样判故障）； 6. airCommand = baseAir + airTrim，钳位到 [0, 100]；fuelPermit=FALSE 时 airCommand 必须为 0.0。说明：baseAir 是外部基础开度给定，本题**不要求** baseAir 自身落在 0..100，只要求最终 airCommand 落在 0..100（baseAir+airTrim 可能为负，此时钳位到 0）； 7. 范围声明：本题只考核**运行阶段**的火焰联锁与氧量修正。预吹扫、点火、火焰建立等启动顺序，以及完整燃烧器安全管理（BMS，含吹扫时的风门要求）不在本题范围内，由其他系统负责。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
runRequestinputBOOL燃烧运行请求
flameOKinputBOOL火焰探测器反馈（TRUE=检测到火焰）
o2ValidinputBOOL氧量分析仪数据有效
o2PVinputREAL烟气含氧量测量值（%）
baseAirinputREAL主风门基础开度给定（%），外部给定，自身不要求在 0..100
resetBtninputBOOL复位按钮（上升沿有效）
fuelPermitoutputBOOL燃料允许（联锁输出）
trimEnableoutputBOOL氧量修正投入（无故障才为 TRUE）
airTrimoutputREAL氧量修正偏置（%），钳位 ±10
airCommandoutputREAL风门开度命令（%），钳位 0..100
tripLatchedoutputBOOL熄火跳闸自保持
o2FaultoutputBOOL氧量分析仪故障（无效或越界）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1运行中失去火焰时，**同一周期**就必须跳闸（不得延迟一拍）。
G((runRequest & !flameOK) -> tripLatched)
safetybounded_checker
S2跳闸一旦置位就自保持；只有"runRequest=FALSE 且 flameOK=FALSE 下的复位上升沿"这一个条件能清除它，其余任何一拍（含按住不复位、条件不满足时按复位）都必须保持。
G((prev(tripLatched) & !(rising(resetBtn) & !runRequest & !flameOK)) -> tripLatched)
safetybounded_checker
S3跳闸状态下绝不允许燃料许可。
G(tripLatched -> !fuelPermit)
safetybounded_checker
S4燃料许可的完整定义：运行请求、火焰正常、未跳闸三者同时成立才允许，缺一不可。
G(fuelPermit <-> (runRequest & flameOK & !tripLatched))
safetybounded_checker
S5无燃料许可时风门命令必须归零（防止停机后残留开度）。
G(!fuelPermit -> airCommand = 0.0)
safetybounded_checker
S6风门命令恒在 0..100 之间。
G((airCommand >= 0.0) & (airCommand <= 100.0))
safetybounded_checker
S7氧量修正偏置恒在 ±10 之间。
G((airTrim >= -10.0) & (airTrim <= 10.0))
safetybounded_checker
S8修正投入的完整定义：有燃料许可且分析仪无故障才投入；故障当拍即整段切除。
G(trimEnable <-> (fuelPermit & !o2Fault))
safetybounded_checker
S9故障判据：数据无效、低于 0 或高于 21 三者任一成立。
G(o2Fault <-> (!o2Valid | o2PV < 0.0 | o2PV > 21.0))
safetybounded_checker
S10修正切除时偏置必须归 0（不得残留上一次的修正量）。
G(!trimEnable -> airTrim = 0.0)
safetybounded_checker
S11只有 o2PV > 7.0 一侧能触到负向限幅：此时偏置被钳在 -10。
G((trimEnable & o2PV > 7.0) -> airTrim = -10.0)
safetybounded_checker
S12o2PV <= 7.0 一侧未达限幅：偏置严格等于 2.0*(2.0-o2PV)。
G((trimEnable & o2PV <= 7.0) -> airTrim = 2.0 * (2.0 - o2PV))
safetybounded_checker
S13修正后合成开度超上限时命令钳在 100，且不改变偏置本身。
G((fuelPermit & baseAir + airTrim > 100.0) -> airCommand = 100.0)
safetybounded_checker
S14修正后合成开度为负时命令钳在 0（baseAir 可以小于 10）。
G((fuelPermit & baseAir + airTrim < 0.0) -> airCommand = 0.0)
safetybounded_checker
S15系数与钳位后的合成值：由仿真用例逐拍核对（净收益/风门数值语义）。—datasimulation
N10-S1611.10补充：熄火运行才触发新跳闸；已有跳闸在合法安全复位沿当拍清除。
G(tripLatched <-> ((runRequest AND !flameOK) OR (prev(tripLatched) AND !(rising(resetBtn) AND !runRequest AND !flameOK))))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1711.10补充：有燃料许可且基础量与修正量之和落在闭区间内，输出必须等于两者之和。
G((fuelPermit AND baseAir + airTrim >= 0.0 AND baseAir + airTrim <= 100.0) -> airCommand = baseAir + airTrim)
safetytest_trace_audit

返回题目目录
self-hard-06

蒸汽母管超压联锁与故障线索诊断（按位编码 + 足迹保留）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 51 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，实现母管超压联锁，并在跳闸时记录**故障线索**。 控制要求： 1. 压力有效判据 pressureValid；>= 4500.0 kPa 判超压； 2. 跳闸条件（任一成立）：runRequest 且 pressureValid 且 pressure >= 4500.0；runRequest 且 NOT pressureValid（数据失效按最坏情况处理）；跳闸与本拍线索同拍生效； 3. tripLatched 自保持；清除条件：resetBtn 上升沿 且 NOT runRequest 且 pressureValid 且 pressure <= 4200.0。4500 与 4200 之间的 300 kPa 是**滞环**：复位门槛比跳闸门槛低 300 kPa，防止压力在阈值附近抖动导致反复跳闸/复位； 4. 线索位（currentCauseMask，WORD 的**低 4 位**，本拍重新计算）： bit0=1 变送器失效（runRequest 且 NOT pressureValid）； bit1=2 减温减压阀卡滞：outletCmd>=50 且 outletFeedback<=10 **连续 3 个执行周期及以上**； bit2=4 燃烧过剩：fuelFlow >= 4.0 且 steamFlow <= 10.0（比较的是接口流量数值，单位 t/h；两个边界都含等于）； bit1/bit2 只在「runRequest 且 pressureValid 且 pressure >= 4500.0」的超压工况下评估；若超压但以上线索都不成立，置 bit3=8（未定位）； NOT runRequest 时线索为 0； 5. 卡滞判据必须是**连续扫描周期**：中途反馈恢复、压力回落到 4500 以下、变送器失效或 runRequest=FALSE，都要把 3 拍计数清零——计数不清零就会把历史误当成“连续”； 6. latchedCauseMask：tripLatched 为 TRUE 时把 currentCauseMask **按位或**并入。注意 WORD 是 **16 位**（DWORD 才是 32 位），本题只用低 4 位（最大 15），16 位足够、不会溢出。该掩码保存的是“本次跳闸期间**全部历史线索的累积**”，**不是**跳闸瞬间的原因快照——跳闸后新出现的线索也会并入，只有合法复位才清零；因此它回答的是“这次跳闸期间一共观察到过哪些现象”，不能把它直接当成唯一的跳闸原因； 7. fuelPermit = runRequest AND NOT tripLatched；diagnosticActive = (currentCauseMask <> 0)。 取数与整定说明： - 本块每个扫描周期执行一次，题目不定义扫描周期时长：“连续 3 拍”指连续3 个执行周期，不承诺固定秒数； - 4500/4200 kPa 与 300 kPa 滞环是该算例的整定值（不据此推定具体装置额定压力或安全阀整定值），只用于考核联锁逻辑，不代表具体机组的最终整定；工程整定须依据母管设计压力与安全阀铭牌； - 燃烧过剩判据 4.0/10.0 t/h 是“燃料流量偏高而蒸汽流量偏低”的算例阈值，同样只作算例参数，不是机组保护定值。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
runRequestinputBOOL运行请求
pressureinputREAL母管压力（kPa）
pressureValidinputBOOL压力变送器数据有效
fuelFlowinputREAL燃料流量（t/h）
steamFlowinputREAL蒸汽流量（t/h）
outletCmdinputINT减温减压阀开度命令（%）
outletFeedbackinputINT减温减压阀开度反馈（%）
resetBtninputBOOL复位按钮（上升沿有效）
tripLatchedoutputBOOL超压跳闸自保持
fuelPermitoutputBOOL燃料许可
currentCauseMaskoutputWORD本周期诊断线索位掩码
latchedCauseMaskoutputWORD跳闸期间线索累积掩码（全历史足迹）
diagnosticActiveoutputBOOL存在有效诊断线索

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1压力变送器失效且仍在运行时，必须当拍跳闸并切除燃料。
G((runRequest & !pressureValid) -> (tripLatched & !fuelPermit))
safetybounded_checker
S2母管压力达到 4500 kPa（数据有效）时必须当拍跳闸。
G((runRequest & pressureValid & pressure >= 4500.0) -> tripLatched)
safetybounded_checker
S3跳闸状态下绝不允许燃料许可。
G(tripLatched -> !fuelPermit)
safetybounded_checker
S4跳闸自保持：没有复位上升沿时不得自行解锁。
G((prev(tripLatched) & !rising(resetBtn)) -> tripLatched)
safetybounded_checker
S4b存在复位上升沿但复位许可条件（停机 + 数据有效 + 压力 <= 4200）不满足时，跳闸也不得解锁。
G((prev(tripLatched) & rising(resetBtn) & (runRequest OR !pressureValid OR pressure > 4200.0)) -> tripLatched)
safetybounded_checker
S5无运行请求时本周期线索掩码必须为 0。
G(!runRequest -> currentCauseMask = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S6压力未越限且数据有效时不产生线索。
G((runRequest & pressureValid & pressure < 4500.0) -> currentCauseMask = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S7诊断有效位与线索掩码一致。
G(diagnosticActive <-> currentCauseMask <> 0)
safetybounded_checker
S8合法复位（上升沿 + 停机 + 数据有效 + 压力 <= 4200）必须同时清除跳闸与线索足迹。
G((rising(resetBtn) & !runRequest & pressureValid & pressure <= 4200.0) -> (!tripLatched & latchedCauseMask = 0))
safetybounded_checker
S9bit3（未定位，掩码恰为 8）只出现在运行中的超压工况。
G(currentCauseMask = 8 -> (runRequest & pressureValid & pressure >= 4500.0))
safetybounded_checker
S10bit2（燃烧过剩）成立时必然满足燃料流量偏高、蒸汽流量偏低的判据。
G((currentCauseMask = 4 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 12 OR currentCauseMask = 14) -> (fuelFlow >= 4.0 & steamFlow <= 10.0))
safetybounded_checker
S11bit1（阀卡滞）成立时必然满足阀命令/反馈的卡滞判据。
G((currentCauseMask = 2 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 10 OR currentCauseMask = 14) -> (outletCmd >= 50 & outletFeedback <= 10))
safetybounded_checker
S12bit0 只在运行中变送器失效时出现，且失效当拍掩码恰为 1。
G((runRequest & !pressureValid) -> currentCauseMask = 1)
safetybounded_checker
S13跳闸期间逐位精确累积：每一历史位等于上一拍历史位或本拍线索位，且仅使用低四位。
G((latchedCauseMask >= 0 AND latchedCauseMask <= 15 AND currentCauseMask >= 0 AND currentCauseMask <= 15) AND (tripLatched -> (((latchedCauseMask = 1 OR latchedCauseMask = 3 OR latchedCauseMask = 5 OR latchedCauseMask = 7 OR latchedCauseMask = 9 OR latchedCauseMask = 11 OR latchedCauseMask = 13 OR latchedCauseMask = 15) <-> (prev((latchedCauseMask = 1 OR latchedCauseMask = 3 OR latchedCauseMask = 5 OR latchedCauseMask = 7 OR latchedCauseMask = 9 OR latchedCauseMask = 11 OR latchedCauseMask = 13 OR latchedCauseMask = 15)) OR (currentCauseMask = 1 OR currentCauseMask = 3 OR currentCauseMask = 5 OR currentCauseMask = 7 OR currentCauseMask = 9 OR currentCauseMask = 11 OR currentCauseMask = 13 OR currentCauseMask = 15))) AND ((latchedCauseMask = 2 OR latchedCauseMask = 3 OR latchedCauseMask = 6 OR latchedCauseMask = 7 OR latchedCauseMask = 10 OR latchedCauseMask = 11 OR latchedCauseMask = 14 OR latchedCauseMask = 15) <-> (prev((latchedCauseMask = 2 OR latchedCauseMask = 3 OR latchedCauseMask = 6 OR latchedCauseMask = 7 OR latchedCauseMask = 10 OR latchedCauseMask = 11 OR latchedCauseMask = 14 OR latchedCauseMask = 15)) OR (currentCauseMask = 2 OR currentCauseMask = 3 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 7 OR currentCauseMask = 10 OR currentCauseMask = 11 OR currentCauseMask = 14 OR currentCauseMask = 15))) AND ((latchedCauseMask = 4 OR latchedCauseMask = 5 OR latchedCauseMask = 6 OR latchedCauseMask = 7 OR latchedCauseMask = 12 OR latchedCauseMask = 13 OR latchedCauseMask = 14 OR latchedCauseMask = 15) <-> (prev((latchedCauseMask = 4 OR latchedCauseMask = 5 OR latchedCauseMask = 6 OR latchedCauseMask = 7 OR latchedCauseMask = 12 OR latchedCauseMask = 13 OR latchedCauseMask = 14 OR latchedCauseMask = 15)) OR (currentCauseMask = 4 OR currentCauseMask = 5 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 7 OR currentCauseMask = 12 OR currentCauseMask = 13 OR currentCauseMask = 14 OR currentCauseMask = 15))) AND ((latchedCauseMask = 8 OR latchedCauseMask = 9 OR latchedCauseMask = 10 OR latchedCauseMask = 11 OR latchedCauseMask = 12 OR latchedCauseMask = 13 OR latchedCauseMask = 14 OR latchedCauseMask = 15) <-> (prev((latchedCauseMask = 8 OR latchedCauseMask = 9 OR latchedCauseMask = 10 OR latchedCauseMask = 11 OR latchedCauseMask = 12 OR latchedCauseMask = 13 OR latchedCauseMask = 14 OR latchedCauseMask = 15)) OR (currentCauseMask = 8 OR currentCauseMask = 9 OR currentCauseMask = 10 OR currentCauseMask = 11 OR currentCauseMask = 12 OR currentCauseMask = 13 OR currentCauseMask = 14 OR currentCauseMask = 15))))))
safetybounded_checker
S14线索位含义、卡滞连续 3 拍判据（中断/压力回落/失效/停机均清零）、足迹累积与复位清零的完整行为。—datasimulation
N10-S1011.10补充：本拍掩码取合法编码；有效超压时必须给出非零诊断，燃烧过剩位与其输入判据等价。
G((currentCauseMask = 0 OR currentCauseMask = 1 OR currentCauseMask = 2 OR currentCauseMask = 4 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 8) AND ((currentCauseMask = 4 OR currentCauseMask = 6) <-> (runRequest AND pressureValid AND pressure >= 4500.0 AND fuelFlow >= 4.0 AND steamFlow <= 10.0)) AND ((runRequest AND pressureValid AND pressure >= 4500.0) -> (currentCauseMask = 2 OR currentCauseMask = 4 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 8)))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1111.10补充：卡滞位当且仅当本拍和此前连续两拍均满足有效超压及阀卡滞输入判据时成立。实例开始前的判据视为假。
G((currentCauseMask = 2 OR currentCauseMask = 6) <-> ((runRequest AND pressureValid AND pressure >= 4500.0 AND outletCmd >= 50 AND outletFeedback <= 10) AND prev((runRequest AND pressureValid AND pressure >= 4500.0 AND outletCmd >= 50 AND outletFeedback <= 10)) AND prev(prev((runRequest AND pressureValid AND pressure >= 4500.0 AND outletCmd >= 50 AND outletFeedback <= 10)))))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1511.10补充：本拍线索掩码仅允许 0、1、2、4、6、8。编码 6 的触发条件另由 S10 与 S11 限制。
G(currentCauseMask = 0 OR currentCauseMask = 1 OR currentCauseMask = 2 OR currentCauseMask = 4 OR currentCauseMask = 6 OR currentCauseMask = 8)
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1611.10补充：燃料许可恰好在请求运行且未锁存跳闸时成立。
G(fuelPermit <-> (runRequest AND NOT tripLatched))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1811.10补充：未跳闸时历史线索应为零。
G(!tripLatched -> latchedCauseMask = 0)
safetytest_trace_audit
S17仅真实跳闸条件可以启动锁存；既有跳闸保持到合法复位沿。
G(tripLatched <-> ((runRequest AND (NOT pressureValid OR pressure >= 4500.0)) OR (prev(tripLatched) AND NOT (rising(resetBtn) AND NOT runRequest AND pressureValid AND pressure <= 4200.0))))
safetybounded_checker

题面

写一个功能块 FB，实现**串级 PI 控制回路**的手/自动无扰切换。 1. 串级结构：外环以温度偏差驱动流量设定，内环以流量偏差驱动阀门开度； 2. 模式：0=手动（操作员直接给定流量设定与阀门开度，两者都跟随手动值）；1=自动（手动给定流量设定 manualFlowSP，内环 PI 自动算阀门）；2=串级（温度环 PI 算流量设定，流量环 PI 算阀门）； 3. 每个扫描周期只执行一次，scanSeconds 为本次周期时长（秒）；控制器系数按**秒**计算：外环 KiT=0.2 (t/h)/(℃·s)、内环 KiF=0.5 %/((t/h)·s)；温度偏差 eT=tempSP-tempPV（℃），流量偏差 eF=flowSP-flowPV（t/h），阀门开度单位 %，因此积分项 accT 的单位是t/h、accF 的单位是 %，增益 1.0 与 2.0 分别对应 ℃→t/h 与 t/h→% 的折算： 外环 trial = accT + 0.2*eT*scanSeconds；flowSP = 1.0*eT + trial；限幅 0..50（t/h）；accT = flowSP - 1.0*eT； 内环 trial = accF + 0.5*eF*scanSeconds；valveCommand = 2.0*eF + trial；限幅 0..100（%）；accF = valveCommand - 2.0*eF； 抗积分饱和用**回算**：acc 取限幅后的实际输出反算，因此限幅期间积分不会越积越多、解除饱和后立即恢复； 4. 模式切换当拍不执行正常 PI 积分，normalMode=FALSE。切到串级 mode=2 时，flowSP 与 valveCommand 均保持上一拍值；切到自动/手动 mode=1/0 时，flowSP 跟随 manualFlowSP，valveCommand 保持上一拍值。连续两拍切换也必须逐拍识别。首次执行与复位、故障拍按初始化/复位/故障规则处理，不适用上一拍输出保持。手动接管后的正常拍允许操作员给定引起阀位突变。 5. 复位 resetBtn 上升沿：整个回路回到初值——预置积分器、输出跟手动值、当拍不运算；按住不放不重复动作； 6. 故障安全：mode 不在 0/1/2，或 scanSeconds<=0.0（时标丢失），或 scanSeconds>10.0（超过 10 s 的扫描周期视为时基异常）→ fault 置位，输出**立即**跟手动值、不积分，并预置积分器；故障期间允许阀位跳变（安全优先，与“无扰切换”区分）； 7. 复位/故障优先于正常运算：同一拍既有复位上升沿又有故障时，输出和积分器按复位规则预置，fault 仍依本拍 mode 和 scanSeconds 如实报告； 8. normalMode 表示“本拍处于正常运算”，即既不在复位拍也不在故障、也不在模式切换/首次执行拍； 9. 模式可以任意跳转（不要求 0→1→2 依次投入；直接 0→2 合法）；但工程上一般建议先投入内环（1）再投入外环（2），这样温度环 PI 的积分器在切换时已有较接近的初值、可减少切换瞬态——这是操作建议，不是本块强制的算法约束。 扫描周期与单位约定：scanSeconds 以秒给出，积分增益按秒定义，算法已显式乘 scanSeconds，不得再次折算。KpT=1.0 (t/h)/℃，KiT=0.2 (t/h)/(℃·s)，KpF=2.0 %/(t/h)，KiF=0.5 %/((t/h)·s)。离散采样周期会影响近似误差与闭环表现，不据此重复调整 Ki。输入契约：所有数值为有限值，manualFlowSP 在 0..50 t/h，manualValve 在 0..100%。 平台适配：原接口 dt（IEC 日期时间类型别名）统一重命名为 scanSeconds；单位秒、类型及原数字断言不变。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
modeinputINT0=手动 1=自动 2=串级（其它值为故障回退）
resetBtninputBOOL复位（上升沿回到初值）
scanSecondsinputREAL本次扫描周期（秒）
tempSPinputREAL温度设定值（℃）
tempPVinputREAL温度测量值（℃）
flowPVinputREAL流量测量值（t/h）
manualFlowSPinputREAL手动流量设定值（t/h）
manualValveinputREAL手动阀门开度取值（%）
flowSPoutputREAL流量设定值输出（t/h，限幅 0..50）
valveCommandoutputREAL阀门开度指令（%，限幅 0..100）
faultoutputBOOL故障（模式越界或 dt 越界）
normalModeoutputBOOL本拍处于正常运算（非复位/故障/切换/首次拍）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1流量设定值始终在 0..50 t/h 之间。
G((flowSP >= 0.0) & (flowSP <= 50.0))
safetybounded_checker
S2阀门指令始终在 0..100 % 之间。
G((valveCommand >= 0.0) & (valveCommand <= 100.0))
safetybounded_checker
S3故障周期必须立即回退到手动：跟手操值且本拍非正常运算。
G(fault -> ((flowSP = manualFlowSP) & (valveCommand = manualValve) & !normalMode))
safetybounded_checker
S4复位上升沿必须让回路回到初值：跟手操值且本拍非正常运算。
G(rising(resetBtn) -> ((flowSP = manualFlowSP) & (valveCommand = manualValve) & !normalMode))
safetybounded_checker
S5手动正常运算周期两个输出分别跟随手动给定。
G((normalMode & (mode = 0)) -> ((flowSP = manualFlowSP) & (valveCommand = manualValve)))
safetybounded_checker
S6自动正常运算周期流量设定跟随 manualFlowSP。
G((normalMode & (mode = 1)) -> (flowSP = manualFlowSP))
safetybounded_checker
S7非首次、无复位/故障的模式切换到自动或手动：流量跟手操，阀位保持，且本拍不正常运算；含连续切换。
G(((prev(TRUE) AND mode <> prev(mode) AND NOT fault AND NOT (resetBtn AND NOT prev(resetBtn))) AND mode <> 2) -> (flowSP = manualFlowSP AND valveCommand = prev(valveCommand) AND NOT normalMode))
sequencingbounded_checker
S8非首次、无复位/故障的模式切换到串级：两输出保持上一拍且不正常运算；不依赖上一拍 normalMode。
G(((prev(TRUE) AND mode <> prev(mode) AND NOT fault AND NOT (resetBtn AND NOT prev(resetBtn))) AND mode = 2) -> (flowSP = prev(flowSP) AND valveCommand = prev(valveCommand) AND NOT normalMode))
sequencingbounded_checker
S9串级/自动 PI 的积分预置、抗积分饱和回算、scanSeconds 校验与手动保持的完整行为。—datasimulation
N10-S1011.10补充：时基或模式非法时且仅此时报告故障；0 到 10 秒之间的正步长均合法。
G(fault <-> (mode < 0 OR mode > 2 OR scanSeconds <= 0.0 OR scanSeconds > 10.0))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1111.10补充：从第二拍起，模式稳定、没有故障且没有复位沿时且仅此时报告正常运算。
G(normalMode <-> (prev(TRUE) & (mode = prev(mode)) & !fault & !rising(resetBtn)))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1211.10补充：首拍按本拍手动流量和阀位预置两个公开输出。
G(!prev(TRUE) -> (flowSP = manualFlowSP AND valveCommand = manualValve))
datatest_trace_audit
N10-S1311.10补充：串级正常运算时，外环由上拍已限幅的流量设定回算积分量，按实际步长更新并裁剪到 0..50。 浮点比较沿用判分容差 10^-6 × max(1, |期望值|)。
G((normalMode AND mode = 2) -> ABS(flowSP - (LIMIT(0.0, 1.0 * (tempSP - tempPV) + ((prev(flowSP) - 1.0 * prev(tempSP - tempPV)) + 0.2 * (tempSP - tempPV) * scanSeconds), 50.0))) <= 0.000001 * MAX(1.0, ABS(LIMIT(0.0, 1.0 * (tempSP - tempPV) + ((prev(flowSP) - 1.0 * prev(tempSP - tempPV)) + 0.2 * (tempSP - tempPV) * scanSeconds), 50.0))))
datatest_trace_audit
N10-S1411.10补充：自动或串级正常运算时，内环由上拍已限幅的阀位回算积分量；本拍内环使用本拍流量设定，按步长更新并裁剪到 0..100。 浮点比较沿用判分容差 10^-6 × max(1, |期望值|)。
G((normalMode AND (mode = 1 OR mode = 2)) -> ABS(valveCommand - (LIMIT(0.0, 2.0 * (flowSP - flowPV) + ((prev(valveCommand) - 2.0 * prev(flowSP - flowPV)) + 0.5 * (flowSP - flowPV) * scanSeconds), 100.0))) <= 0.000001 * MAX(1.0, ABS(LIMIT(0.0, 2.0 * (flowSP - flowPV) + ((prev(valveCommand) - 2.0 * prev(flowSP - flowPV)) + 0.5 * (flowSP - flowPV) * scanSeconds), 100.0))))
datatest_trace_audit

默认组 · sequence · 组内依次执行，同一实例保留状态 · 40 条

返回题目目录
self-medium-01

配料配方装载与校验（数组越界与浮点区间）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 23 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，从配方库中按槽位装载配方并校验。 控制要求： 1. recipe 是 8 个槽位的配方数组，槽位 recipeID = 0 表示空槽； 2. selSlot 是操作员选中的槽位，合法范围 1..8；**越界时必须判为非法，不得访问数组外**； 3. loadCmd 的**上升沿**触发一次装载；按住不放不得重复装载； 4. 校验规则（**全部满足**才算 valid）：槽位非空；totalRatio 在 99.0..101.0 之间（含端点）；temperature 在 30.0..180.0 之间（含端点）； 5. 校验通过时 valid := TRUE 且 loadedID 更新为该槽位的 recipeID；校验失败时 valid := FALSE 且 loadedID 归 0； 6. running 为 TRUE 表示正在生产，此时装载命令无效，loadedID 保持原值。 平台约定：内部结构体临时变量避免使用单字母 r；当前平台对原写法报告词法冲突，接口保持原样。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
recipeinputARRAY[1..8] OF ST_Recipe配方槽位 1..8，槽位 recipeID=0 表示空
recipe[].recipeIDinputINT配方号，0 表示空槽
recipe[].totalRatioinputREAL配方总比例，合法 99.0..101.0
recipe[].temperatureinputREAL工艺温度，合法 30.0..180.0
selSlotinputINT操作员选中的槽位，合法范围 1..8
loadCmdinputBOOL装载命令（上升沿触发一次装载）
runninginputBOOL当前是否处于生产状态
validoutputBOOL配方校验通过
loadedIDoutputINT已装载的配方号，无配方时为 0

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1收到合法槽位的装载沿且未生产时，若选中空槽，valid 为 FALSE 且 loadedID 清零。
G(!running & rising(loadCmd) & (selSlot >= 1) & (selSlot <= 8) & (recipe[selSlot].recipeID = 0) -> (!valid AND loadedID = 0))
safetybounded_checker
S2总配比越界（totalRatio < 99 或 > 101）时装载必须判为非法。
G((!running AND rising(loadCmd) AND selSlot >= 1 AND selSlot <= 8 AND (recipe[selSlot].totalRatio < 99.0 OR recipe[selSlot].totalRatio > 101.0)) -> !valid)
safetybounded_checker
S3温度越界（temperature < 30 或 > 180）时装载必须判为非法。
G((!running AND rising(loadCmd) AND selSlot >= 1 AND selSlot <= 8 AND (recipe[selSlot].temperature < 30.0 OR recipe[selSlot].temperature > 180.0)) -> !valid)
safetybounded_checker
S4连续两拍处于生产状态时，本拍编号保持上一拍值。进入生产的首拍另由 N10-S4 覆盖。
G((prev(running) AND running) -> loadedID = prev(loadedID))
safetybounded_checker
S5槽位越界（selSlot < 1 或 > 8）时必须判为非法，不得访问数组外内存。
G(!running & rising(loadCmd) & ((selSlot < 1) | (selSlot > 8)) -> !valid)
safetybounded_checker
S6装载命令只在上升沿生效，电平保持期间不得重复装载。
G(loadCmd & prev(loadCmd) -> loadedID = prev(loadedID))
sequencingbounded_checker
N10-S411.10补充：本拍处于生产状态时，loadedID 保持上一拍的值，包括刚进入生产的第一拍。
G(running -> loadedID = prev(loadedID))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S711.10补充：接收装载沿且下标合法时，有效位等价于槽非空、配比和温度均在闭区间。
G((!running AND rising(loadCmd) AND selSlot >= 1 AND selSlot <= 8) -> (valid <-> (recipe[selSlot].recipeID <> 0 AND recipe[selSlot].totalRatio >= 99.0 AND recipe[selSlot].totalRatio <= 101.0 AND recipe[selSlot].temperature >= 30.0 AND recipe[selSlot].temperature <= 180.0)))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S811.10补充：有效装载回传选中槽的实际编号；无效装载清零编号。
G((!running AND rising(loadCmd)) -> ((!valid -> loadedID = 0) AND ((valid AND selSlot >= 1 AND selSlot <= 8) -> loadedID = recipe[selSlot].recipeID)))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S911.10补充：没有接受装载沿时，ID 和有效位都保持上拍。
G((running OR !rising(loadCmd)) -> ((valid <-> prev(valid)) AND loadedID = prev(loadedID)))
safetytest_trace_audit
F13-S1选中的槽位为空（recipeID = 0）时，装载必须判为非法，且不得改变已装载的 loadedID。
G(!running & rising(loadCmd) & (selSlot >= 1) & (selSlot <= 8) & (recipe[selSlot].recipeID = 0) -> !valid)
safetybounded_checker
F13-S2总配比越界（totalRatio < 99 或 > 101）时装载必须判为非法。
G(!running & rising(loadCmd) & (selSlot >= 1) & (selSlot <= 8) & (recipe[selSlot].totalRatio < 99.0) -> !valid)
safetybounded_checker
F13-S3温度越界（temperature < 30 或 > 180）时装载必须判为非法。
G(!running & rising(loadCmd) & (selSlot >= 1) & (selSlot <= 8) & (recipe[selSlot].temperature > 180.0) -> !valid)
safetybounded_checker

self-medium-02

模拟量滑动平均滤波（窗口未满与 FIFO 淘汰）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 30 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，对模拟量采样值做 8 点滑动平均滤波。 控制要求： 1. 每个扫描周期，若 enable 为 TRUE，把 rawValue 压入长度为 8 的环形缓冲区，并用缓冲区内**实际已有**的样本计算算术平均，输出到 filteredValue； 2. 缓冲区**未填满时不得用 0 填充**：只有 3 个样本时就是这 3 个的平均； 3. 缓冲区填满后，每来一个新样本挤掉最老的一个（先进先出）； 4. sampleCount 输出当前缓冲区内的有效样本数（0..8）； 5. 当样本数达到 8 时 windowFull 置 TRUE，之后保持 TRUE； 6. enable 为 FALSE 时清空缓冲区（sampleCount 归 0、windowFull 归 FALSE），但 filteredValue 保持上一次的计算结果不变。 每个新启用时段独立计数；同一时段第 8 个样本首次置满，第 9 个样本开始淘汰最老值。停用时清零计数和满标志，保持末次均值。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
enableinputBOOL滤波使能，FALSE 时清空历史
rawValueinputREAL本周期原始采样值
filteredValueoutputREAL滤波输出
windowFulloutputBOOL历史窗口已填满 8 个样本后置 TRUE
sampleCountoutputINT当前窗口内有效样本数 0..8

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1输出等于最近最多 8 个有效样本的均值；满窗后按先入先出淘汰最老值，以连续样本序列检查第 8、9 拍。—datasimulation
S2windowFull 当且仅当本周期结束后已有 8 个样本。
G(windowFull <-> sampleCount = 8)
safetybounded_checker
S3enable 拉低后，sampleCount 必须在**当周期**归零，窗口同时退出满载。
G(!enable -> sampleCount = 0 & !windowFull)
safetybounded_checker
S4样本数在 0..8 之间；使能时按拍增加至 8，已满时保持 8。
G((sampleCount >= 0 AND sampleCount <= 8) AND (enable AND prev(sampleCount < 8) -> sampleCount = prev(sampleCount) + 1) AND (enable AND prev(sampleCount = 8) -> sampleCount = 8))
safetybounded_checker
S5停用当拍与持续停用均保持上次输出，不随新的 rawValue 变化。
G(!enable -> filteredValue = prev(filteredValue))
safetybounded_checker
S6新启用时段的第一个样本直接输出原值。
G((enable AND sampleCount = 1) -> filteredValue = rawValue)
safetybounded_checker
S7连续第二个样本输出两个有效样本的算术平均。
G((enable AND sampleCount = 2) -> filteredValue = (prev(rawValue) + rawValue) / 2.0)
safetybounded_checker

self-medium-03

闸门电机有限状态机（四状态与安全互锁）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 36 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

实现闸门功能块 FB，状态为 IDLE、OPENING、CLOSING、FAULT。上电为 IDLE，两按钮的初始前值为 FALSE。输入在每次扫描开始时采样，输出表示本拍结束时的命令。采用以下课程用防夹规则。 1. 无障碍的 IDLE 中，只有开按钮出现新上升沿、关按钮为 FALSE 且未开到位才开始上升。关方向对称。双键同按不启动。启动当拍即给出对应电机命令；松开按钮继续运行。在运行中忽略方向按钮。 2. 无障碍时，到达本方向限位则当拍停机并回到 IDLE。另一方向限位不妨碍运动。任一时刻两个电机方向互斥。 3. 任意状态检测到障碍，当拍关闭两个方向并进入FAULT，errorFlag置TRUE；优先于限位、运动与复位。 4. FAULT 锁存 errorFlag 并停机。清除须在无障碍时让双键组合从未同时按下变为同时按下；有障碍时按住双键，不能在障碍解除那一拍自动复位。复位仅回到 IDLE，复位当拍两方向都停止，之后按规则 1 重启。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
openBtninputBOOL开门按钮（上升沿有效）
closeBtninputBOOL关门按钮（上升沿有效）
openLimitinputBOOL开到位限位开关
closeLimitinputBOOL关到位限位开关
obstacleinputBOOL防夹物体检测
motorUpoutputBOOL电机上升（开门）输出
motorDownoutputBOOL电机下降（关门）输出
errorFlagoutputBOOL故障锁存（需人工复位后清除）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1两方向不可同时启动。
G(!(motorUp AND motorDown))
safetybounded_checker
S2有障碍的扫描周期两个方向都停。
G(obstacle -> (!motorUp AND !motorDown))
safetybounded_checker
S3达到本方向限位就停该方向。
G((openLimit -> !motorUp) AND (closeLimit -> !motorDown))
safetybounded_checker
S4故障有效时两个方向都停。
G(errorFlag -> (!motorUp AND !motorDown))
safetybounded_checker
S5已锁存的故障，只有无障碍且双键组合出现新上升沿时才可清除。
G((prev(errorFlag) AND !(!obstacle AND rising(openBtn AND closeBtn))) -> errorFlag)
sequencingbounded_checker
S6下降遇障当拍置故障并停机。
G((prev(motorDown) AND obstacle) -> (errorFlag AND !motorUp AND !motorDown))
sequencingbounded_checker
S7上一拍有障碍，若无新的方向按键沿，两个输出继续保持停止。
G((prev(obstacle) AND !rising(openBtn) AND !rising(closeBtn)) -> (!motorUp AND !motorDown))
sequencingbounded_checker
S8上升时遇障或障碍连续两拍，故障当拍置位。
G((obstacle AND (prev(motorUp) OR prev(obstacle))) -> errorFlag)
sequencingbounded_checker
S9在无障碍时人工复位故障：清除报警，当拍仍停机。
G((prev(errorFlag) AND !obstacle AND rising(openBtn AND closeBtn)) -> (!errorFlag AND !motorUp AND !motorDown))
sequencingbounded_checker
S10空闲状态收到合法开门新指令，当拍上升。
G((!prev(motorUp) AND !prev(motorDown) AND !prev(errorFlag) AND !obstacle AND rising(openBtn) AND !closeBtn AND !openLimit) -> motorUp)
sequencingbounded_checker
S11空闲状态收到合法关门新指令，当拍下降。
G((!prev(motorUp) AND !prev(motorDown) AND !prev(errorFlag) AND !obstacle AND rising(closeBtn) AND !openBtn AND !closeLimit) -> motorDown)
sequencingbounded_checker
S12运动命令必须来自该方向的合法新启动或运行延续；障碍或本方向限位立即停机。
G(motorUp <-> (!obstacle AND !openLimit AND (prev(motorUp) OR (!prev(motorUp) AND !prev(motorDown) AND !prev(errorFlag) AND rising(openBtn) AND !closeBtn))))
safetybounded_checker
S13运动命令必须来自该方向的合法新启动或运行延续；障碍或本方向限位立即停机。
G(motorDown <-> (!obstacle AND !closeLimit AND (prev(motorDown) OR (!prev(motorUp) AND !prev(motorDown) AND !prev(errorFlag) AND rising(closeBtn) AND !openBtn))))
safetybounded_checker
S14任意障碍置故障；障碍解除后仅双键组合新沿可复位。
G(errorFlag <-> (obstacle OR (prev(errorFlag) AND !rising(openBtn AND closeBtn))))
safetybounded_checker

功能用例

默认组 · sequence · 组内依次执行，同一实例保留状态 · 31 条

用例扫描拍数初态／当条预置常量输入当条输入必需期望 expected断言范围说明

返回题目目录
self-medium-04

定量灌装阀控制（浮点累计与计数去抖）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 35 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，控制定量灌装工位。初始阀门关闭、无待确认瓶，fillsToday 从 0 开始。startBtn 和 resetBtn 检测上升沿，按钮的上电前值按 FALSE 处理；doneSensor 按本拍电平读取。每拍按下列顺序执行，输出在拍末观察。 1. resetBtn 出现上升沿时优先复位：清零计数和累计体积，关阀并清除待确认，忽略本拍的启动、流量和确认。 2. 空闲或等待确认时，startBtn 出现上升沿就开始新瓶，清零本瓶累计并开阀。若上一瓶仍待确认，则先取消上一瓶的待确认，再处理新瓶。灌装中出现新的启动沿不重置本瓶。 3. 正在灌装就加入本拍 flowVol，启动拍也计入。累计体积达到或超过本拍 targetVol 时立即关阀并令本瓶待确认。 4. 本拍关阀后立即读取 doneSensor；若已为 TRUE，本拍确认并计数。若为 FALSE，则在后续首次读取 TRUE 的拍确认（除非先复位或开始新瓶）。每次确认最多记一瓶，完成后立即清除待确认；传感器持续为 TRUE 不重复计数。若新瓶启动当拍即达标，本拍确认的是新瓶。 5. 每次确认令有符号 INT 型 fillsToday 增加 1；原计数为 32767 时，仍完成并清除本次确认，计数保持 32767。复位后从零重新累计。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
startBtninputBOOL启动按钮（上升沿有效）
resetBtninputBOOL复位按钮（上升沿有效）
doneSensorinputBOOL满瓶检测传感器
targetVolinputREAL目标灌装体积 mL
flowVolinputREAL本周期累计流量增量 mL
valveOpenoutputBOOL灌装阀开命令
fillsTodayoutputINT今日完成瓶数

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1复位按钮上升沿必须在**当周期**把今日计数清零。
G(rising(resetBtn) -> fillsToday = 0)
safetybounded_checker
S2阀门本拍由关变开时，本拍必须有启动按钮上升沿；上电前阀门关闭。
G((!prev(valveOpen) AND valveOpen) -> rising(startBtn))
sequencingbounded_checker
S3除复位那一拍外，计数单调不减（不得回退）。
G(!rising(resetBtn) -> fillsToday >= prev(fillsToday))
safetybounded_checker
S6每拍计数最多增加 1（不得因阀抖动重复计数）。
G(fillsToday <= prev(fillsToday) + 1)
safetybounded_checker
S4计数只发生在满瓶确认之后：计数增加的那一拍 doneSensor 必须为真。
G(fillsToday > prev(fillsToday) -> doneSensor)
safetybounded_checker
S5累计流量达到目标体积后，灌装阀必须在当周期关闭（涉及内部累计量，只在用例中校验）。—datasimulation
S7复位沿优先，关阀并归零。
G(rising(resetBtn) -> !valveOpen)
safetybounded_checker
S8新瓶启动拍即计入流量，未达标开阀、达标当拍关阀。
G((!rising(resetBtn) AND rising(startBtn) AND !prev(valveOpen)) -> (valveOpen <-> flowVol < targetVol))
safetybounded_checker
S9运行中本拍无新增流量且目标不变，保持开阀。
G((!rising(resetBtn) AND prev(valveOpen) AND flowVol = 0.0 AND targetVol = prev(targetVol)) -> valveOpen)
safetybounded_checker
S10非复位关阀且关阀拍尚未确认，下一拍没有复位或新启动而确认时，未饱和计数加一。
G((prev(falling(valveOpen) AND !rising(resetBtn) AND !doneSensor) AND !rising(resetBtn) AND !rising(startBtn) AND doneSensor AND !prev(fillsToday = 32767)) -> fillsToday = prev(fillsToday) + 1)
safetybounded_checker
S11复位已清除待确认标志；其后未新启动时，确认信号不能计瓶。
G((prev(rising(resetBtn)) AND !rising(resetBtn) AND !rising(startBtn)) -> fillsToday = prev(fillsToday))
safetybounded_checker
S12首次启动贡献至少半个目标体积，下一拍无复位且不倒流仍开阀；第三拍再次启动且补入至少另一半时，须按原累计关阀。
G((prev(prev(rising(startBtn) AND !prev(valveOpen) AND !rising(resetBtn) AND valveOpen AND flowVol + flowVol >= targetVol)) AND prev(!startBtn AND !rising(resetBtn) AND valveOpen AND flowVol >= 0.0 AND targetVol = prev(targetVol)) AND rising(startBtn) AND !rising(resetBtn) AND targetVol = prev(targetVol) AND flowVol + flowVol >= targetVol) -> !valveOpen)
safetybounded_checker
S13本拍结束时新瓶仍在开阀灌装，确认信号不能给上一瓶计数。
G((!rising(resetBtn) AND valveOpen) -> fillsToday = prev(fillsToday))
safetybounded_checker
S14此前开阀、本拍关阀，或新瓶启动拍直接达标关闭时，未复位且本拍已有确认信号，未饱和的计数当拍加一；饱和时保持上限。
G((!rising(resetBtn) AND doneSensor AND (falling(valveOpen) OR (rising(startBtn) AND !prev(valveOpen) AND flowVol >= targetVol))) -> ((prev(fillsToday < 32767) -> fillsToday = prev(fillsToday) + 1) AND (prev(fillsToday = 32767) -> fillsToday = 32767)))
sequencingbounded_checker

self-medium-05

合格蒸汽产量与净收益累计（闭区间判定 + 数据故障冻结）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 30 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，按扫描周期累计锅炉的合格蒸汽产量、燃料消耗与净收益。 控制要求： 1. 合格判据：steamTemp 在 [447.0, 453.0] 且 steamPressure 在 [3750.0, 3850.0]（**闭区间**，端点合格；温度高于 453、压力低于 3750 或高于 3850 都判不合格）； 2. 本周期合格且 enable=TRUE 且无数据故障时，qualifiedTon += steamFlow * scanSeconds / 1000.0（流量按 kg/s、周期按 s 计，kg/s × s ÷ 1000 = t）；无论是否合格，只要 enable 且无故障，fuelTon += fuelFlow * scanSeconds / 1000.0； 3. 数据故障判据：scanSeconds <= 0.0 或 scanSeconds > 10.0（含恰好 10.0 合法、10.0 以上非法）或 steamFlow 不在 [0,90] 或 fuelFlow 不在 [0,6]（负流量同样判故障）；故障拍不累计；仅在没有复位上升沿时保持累计值； 4. resetBtn 上升沿：两个累计量清零，且**当拍不再累计**。复位是边沿命令、不是保持命令：按住的时长不影响判定，每拍只认有没有新的上升沿。**复位优先于故障冻结**：同一拍同时出现复位上升沿与数据故障时，先执行清零，清零结果生效（故障位仍如实报出，但累计量不得停留在旧值）； 5. revenue = qualifiedTon * 280.0，cost = fuelTon * 1500.0，netProfit = revenue - cost，三者每拍都要更新（即使不累计）。单价单位：蒸汽 280 元/t，燃料 1500 元/t，配 kg 换算后金额单位为元； 6. qualifiedTon、fuelTon、revenue、cost 必须非负；netProfit **允许为负**（烧燃料不产汽时会亏损）。复位上升沿优先于禁用及数据故障。当拍清零且不累计。无复位沿时禁用或故障冻结累计量；判定位和金额仍按当拍更新。 平台适配：原接口 dt（IEC 日期时间类型别名）统一重命名为 scanSeconds；单位秒、类型及原数字断言不变。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
enableinputBOOL计量使能
resetBtninputBOOL清零按钮（上升沿有效）
scanSecondsinputLREAL本周期时长（s），合法 0 < dt <= 10
steamFlowinputLREAL蒸汽流量（kg/s），合法 0..90
fuelFlowinputLREAL燃料流量（kg/s），合法 0..6
steamTempinputLREAL蒸汽温度（℃）
steamPressureinputLREAL蒸汽压力（kPa）
qualifiedoutputBOOL本周期蒸汽是否合格
dataFaultoutputBOOL数据故障（参数越界）
qualifiedTonoutputLREAL累计合格蒸汽产量（t）
fuelTonoutputLREAL累计燃料消耗（t）
revenueoutputLREAL累计收入（元）
costoutputLREAL累计成本（元）
netProfitoutputLREAL累计净收益（元）

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1合格判据：温度 447..453 ℃ 且 压力 3750..3850 kPa（闭区间）。
G(qualified <-> (steamTemp >= 447.0 & steamTemp <= 453.0 & steamPressure >= 3750.0 & steamPressure <= 3850.0))
safetybounded_checker
S2产量、燃料、收入、成本四项累计量永远非负（净收益不在此列，允许亏损）。
G((qualifiedTon >= 0.0) & (fuelTon >= 0.0) & (revenue >= 0.0) & (cost >= 0.0))
safetybounded_checker
S3复位上升沿当拍把两个累计量清零。
G(rising(resetBtn) -> (qualifiedTon = 0.0 & fuelTon = 0.0))
safetybounded_checker
S4数据故障判据：scanSeconds 不在 (0,10] 或流量越界。
G(dataFault <-> (scanSeconds <= 0.0 | scanSeconds > 10.0 | steamFlow < 0.0 | steamFlow > 90.0 | fuelFlow < 0.0 | fuelFlow > 6.0))
safetybounded_checker
S5数据故障或未使能时，累计燃料在本拍保持不变。
G((!rising(resetBtn) & (!enable | dataFault)) -> fuelTon = prev(fuelTon))
safetybounded_checker
S6数据故障且未复位时，累计产量在本拍保持不变。
G((dataFault & !rising(resetBtn)) -> qualifiedTon = prev(qualifiedTon))
safetybounded_checker
S7金额三式恒成立：收入=产量×280、成本=燃料×1500、净收益=收入-成本。
G((revenue = qualifiedTon * 280.0) & (cost = fuelTon * 1500.0) & (netProfit = revenue - cost))
safetybounded_checker
S9复位上升沿优先于故障冻结：复位与数据故障同拍时，累计量仍必须清零。
G((rising(resetBtn) & dataFault) -> (qualifiedTon = 0.0 & fuelTon = 0.0))
safetybounded_checker
S10公开输入和累计输出的增量及收入成本关系；复位拍不累计，浮点容差 1e-6。
G(ABS(revenue - qualifiedTon * 280.0) <= 0.000001 AND ABS(cost - fuelTon * 1500.0) <= 0.000001 AND ABS(netProfit - (revenue - cost)) <= 0.000001 AND ((enable AND NOT dataFault AND NOT (resetBtn AND NOT prev(resetBtn))) AND qualified -> ABS(qualifiedTon - (prev(qualifiedTon) + steamFlow * scanSeconds / 1000.0)) <= 0.000001) AND ((enable AND NOT dataFault AND NOT (resetBtn AND NOT prev(resetBtn))) -> ABS(fuelTon - (prev(fuelTon) + fuelFlow * scanSeconds / 1000.0)) <= 0.000001))
databounded_checker
N10-S1111.10补充：没有复位沿且使能、数据有效时，本拍燃料累计量必须按燃料流量和实际步长增加。
G((!rising(resetBtn) AND enable AND !dataFault) -> fuelTon = prev(fuelTon) + fuelFlow * scanSeconds / 1000.0)
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1211.10补充：合格、使能且数据有效的非复位拍按蒸汽流量和步长增加合格吨数；其余非复位拍保持上拍的合格吨数。
G(!rising(resetBtn) -> (((enable AND !dataFault AND qualified) -> qualifiedTon = prev(qualifiedTon) + steamFlow * scanSeconds / 1000.0) AND ((!enable OR dataFault OR !qualified) -> qualifiedTon = prev(qualifiedTon))))
safetytest_trace_audit

self-medium-06

温度模糊控制与输出钳位（重心法 + 历史误差清除）

FUNCTION_BLOCK · 23 条功能用例 · 题目 JSON

题面

写一个功能块 FB，用九条规则的温度模糊控制器计算加热功率。 控制要求： 1. e = setpoint - temperature；de = (e - ePrev) / scanSeconds，**首拍**或历史被清除时 de = 0； 2. x = clamp(e / 10.0, -1, 1)，y = clamp(de / 2.0, -1, 1)。量程说明：±10 ℃ 与 ±2 ℃/s 是本演示的满量程假设（对应温度回路约 ±10 ℃、变化率 ±2 ℃/s 的振荡工况；按幅值 ±10 ℃、周期 31.4 s 估算，变化率峰值约 2.0 ℃/s，该假设在演示工况下自洽），换到真实装置应按实际量程重新整定； 3. 隶属度：μN(z)=max(-z,0)、μZ(z)=1-|z|、μP(z)=max(z,0)； 4. 规则权重 w(i,j)=μi(x)×μj(y)，行对应误差 x，列对应变化率 y。九条规则的单点输出：N×N=-30、N×Z=-20、N×P=-10、Z×N=-10、Z×Z=0、Z×P=10、P×N=10、P×Z=20、P×P=30；correction = Σ(w·c)/Σw（重心法），Σw=0 时 correction=0； 5. power = clamp(bias + correction, 0, 100)，**correction 不钳位**； 6. fault = NOT sensorValid OR scanSeconds <= 0.0 OR scanSeconds > 10.0；fault、enable=FALSE 或 resetBtn **上升沿**时：power=0、correction=0，并清除历史（ePrev=0、历史标志=FALSE）。复位是边沿命令、不是停机保持命令：按住的时长不影响判定，每拍只认有没有新的上升沿；按住复位期间输出的 0 只在上升沿那一拍成立，之后回到正常模糊计算（历史已清除，所以恢复后的首拍按 de=0 处理）。“首拍/复位后/故障或禁用后恢复的首拍”指历史标志为 FALSE 的那一拍，该拍 ePrev 不参与，de 一律取 0；下一拍起才用正常差分。 平台适配：原接口 dt（IEC 日期时间类型别名）统一重命名为 scanSeconds；单位秒、类型及原数字断言不变。

判分接口

变量或字段方向类型说明
enableinputBOOL自动投入
resetBtninputBOOL复位（上升沿清除历史误差）
sensorValidinputBOOL温度传感器数据有效
setpointinputREAL温度设定值（℃）
temperatureinputREAL温度测量值（℃）
scanSecondsinputREAL本周期时长（s），合法 0 < dt <= 10
biasinputREAL基础输出（%）
poweroutputREAL加热功率输出（%），钳位 0..100
correctionoutputREAL模糊修正量（%）
faultoutputBOOL传感器或参数故障

规约

编号性质公式类别数据标注的核对方式
S1加热功率恒在 0..100 之间。
G((power >= 0.0) & (power <= 100.0))
safetybounded_checker
S2模糊修正量恒在 ±30 之间（九个单点输出量的极值）。
G((correction >= -30.0) & (correction <= 30.0))
safetybounded_checker
S3未投入、复位上升沿或故障时，输出与修正必须归零。
G((!enable | rising(resetBtn) | fault) -> (power = 0.0 & correction = 0.0))
safetybounded_checker
S4故障判据：传感器无效或 scanSeconds 越界。
G(fault <-> (!sensorValid | scanSeconds <= 0.0 | scanSeconds > 10.0))
safetybounded_checker
S5传感器无效必然判故障（不允许用坏数据继续闭环）。
G(!sensorValid -> fault)
safetybounded_checker
S7故障/禁用之后重新计算的首拍，历史必须已清除：误差为正且超过满量程时只踩 P×Z 一条规则，修正恰为 +20（若历史未清，de 会带出别的值）。
G(((prev(fault) | prev(!enable)) & enable & !fault & !rising(resetBtn) & setpoint - temperature >= 10.0) -> correction = 20.0)
safetybounded_checker
S8故障/禁用之后重新计算的首拍，误差为 0 时修正必须为 0（历史未清则会因 de≠0 给出非零修正）。
G(((prev(fault) | prev(!enable)) & enable & !fault & !rising(resetBtn) & setpoint = temperature) -> correction = 0.0)
safetybounded_checker
S9复位不是保持命令：按住复位（无新上升沿）时不得继续强制清零，必须按正常模糊逻辑出功率。
G((resetBtn & prev(resetBtn) & !fault & enable & (bias > 0.0) & setpoint - temperature >= 10.0) -> power > 0.0)
safetybounded_checker
S10功率为基础输出与修正合成后的钳位；故障、停用或复位上升沿当拍为零。
G(((NOT enable OR fault OR (resetBtn AND NOT prev(resetBtn))) -> power = 0.0) AND (NOT (NOT enable OR fault OR (resetBtn AND NOT prev(resetBtn))) -> ABS(power - LIMIT(0.0, bias + correction, 100.0)) <= 0.000001))
databounded_checker
N10-S1111.10补充：有效且使能的非复位拍，输出功率等于基础功率加修正值后裁剪到 0 至 100。
G((enable AND !fault AND !rising(resetBtn)) -> power = LIMIT(0.0, bias + correction, 100.0))
safetytest_trace_audit
N10-S1211.10补充：有效首拍或历史在上拍被清除后，变化率取零，修正量应等于20倍归一化误差。 浮点比较沿用判分容差 10^-6 × max(1, |期望值|)。
G(((enable AND !fault AND !rising(resetBtn)) AND !(prev(enable AND !fault) AND !prev(rising(resetBtn)))) -> ABS(correction - (20.0 * LIMIT(-1.0, (setpoint - temperature) / 10.0, 1.0))) <= 0.000001 * MAX(1.0, ABS(20.0 * LIMIT(-1.0, (setpoint - temperature) / 10.0, 1.0))))
datatest_trace_audit
N10-S1311.10补充：具有上一拍有效历史时，九规则乘积加权重心等价于20x+10y，x、y均限于[-1,1]；按本拍有效步长计算误差变化率。 浮点比较沿用判分容差 10^-6 × max(1, |期望值|)。
G(((enable AND !fault AND !rising(resetBtn)) AND (prev(enable AND !fault) AND !prev(rising(resetBtn)))) -> ABS(correction - ((20.0 * LIMIT(-1.0, (setpoint - temperature) / 10.0, 1.0) + 10.0 * LIMIT(-1.0, (((setpoint - temperature) - prev(setpoint - temperature)) / scanSeconds) / 2.0, 1.0)))) <= 0.000001 * MAX(1.0, ABS((20.0 * LIMIT(-1.0, (setpoint - temperature) / 10.0, 1.0) + 10.0 * LIMIT(-1.0, (((setpoint - temperature) - prev(setpoint - temperature)) / scanSeconds) / 2.0, 1.0)))))
datatest_trace_audit

{"correction": 9.0, "power": 59.0}
列出字段四条规则同时激活，其中 Z×Z 的单点输出为零：e=2、de=1、dt=2 → x=0.2/y=0.5；隶属度 μZ(x)=0.8、μP(x)=0.2、μZ(y)=0.5、μP(y)=0.5 → Z×Z(0.8×0.5×0=0)、Z×P(0.8×0.5×10=4)、P×Z(0.2×0.5×20=2)、P×P(0.2×0.5×30=3)，权重和 0.4+0.4+0.1+0.1=1 → 修正 (0+4+2+3)/1 = 9.0
